private const val MY_LIST_ROUTE = "myList"

object DrawerActions {
    val onSetGroupingMode = MutableSharedFlow<LiveCatalogViewModel.GroupingMode>(replay = 0)
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun AppNavigation(startDestination: String = Routes.LiveTv.route) {
    val navController = rememberNavController()
    val sharedCatalogViewModel: SharedCatalogViewModel = viewModel()
    val playerViewModel: M3uLiveTvViewModel = viewModel()

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        sharedCatalogViewModel.loadData()
    }

    val drawerState = rememberDrawerState(DrawerValue.Closed)
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    val navBackStackEntry by navController.currentBackStackEntryAsState()
    val currentRoute = navBackStackEntry?.destination?.route

    val isOnMovies = currentRoute == Routes.Movies.route || currentRoute?.startsWith("movieDetail") == true || currentRoute?.startsWith("m3u_movie_detail") == true
    val isOnSeries = currentRoute == Routes.Series.route || currentRoute?.startsWith("seriesDetail") == true || currentRoute?.startsWith("m3u_series_detail") == true

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val activity = context as? android.app.Activity
    val isTvDevice = remember { context.isTv() }

    LaunchedEffect(currentRoute) {
        if (currentRoute != null) {
            if (currentRoute != Routes.LiveTv.route && currentRoute != "livePlayer") {
                PlayerHolder.liveIsBuffering = false
            }
            if (!isOnMovies && !isOnSeries) {
                VodPlayerHolder.player?.let { player -> player.stop(); player.clearMediaItems() }
                VodPlayerHolder.isBuffering = false
            }
        }
    }

    val onOpenDrawer: () -> Unit = { scope.launch { drawerState.open() } }

    val onNavigate: (String) -> Unit = { route ->
        navController.navigate(route) {
            popUpTo(navController.graph.findStartDestination().id) { saveState = true }
            launchSingleTop = true
            restoreState = true
        }
        scope.launch { drawerState.close() }
    }

    val isMainScreen = currentRoute == Routes.LiveTv.route ||
            currentRoute == Routes.Movies.route ||
            currentRoute == Routes.Series.route ||
            currentRoute == MY_LIST_ROUTE ||
            currentRoute == Routes.Profile.route

    val isFullscreen by remember { derivedStateOf { PlayerUiState.isFullscreen } }
    val isCatalogDetailOpen = M3uCatalogNavigationState.isDetailVisible

    val allowDrawerGesture = !isTvDevice && isMainScreen && !isFullscreen && !isCatalogDetailOpen

    LaunchedEffect(allowDrawerGesture) {
        if (!allowDrawerGesture && drawerState.isOpen) {
            drawerState.close()
        }
    }

    // ✅ Detectar si estamos en pantallas que deben mostrar la barra lateral en TV
    val showTvSideBar = isTvDevice && isMainScreen && !isFullscreen && !isCatalogDetailOpen

    val navHostContent: @Composable () -> Unit = {
        NavHost(
            navController = navController,
            startDestination = startDestination,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
        ) {
            composable("login") { LoginScreen(navController = navController) }
            composable("splash") { SplashScreen(navController = navController) }

            composable(Routes.LiveTv.route, enterTransition = { EnterTransition.None }, exitTransition = { ExitTransition.None }) {
                val catalogViewModel: LiveCatalogViewModel = viewModel()
                val channels by catalogViewModel.channels.collectAsState()
                LaunchedEffect(channels) { playerViewModel.allChannels = channels }

                LiveTvHomeScreen(
                    catalogViewModel = catalogViewModel,
                    onNavigateToPlayer = { channel ->
                        playerViewModel.requestPlayback(channel)
                        navController.navigate("livePlayer")
                        ClosePerfTracker.track("NAVIGATE_TO_PLAYER")
                    },
                    onOpenDrawer = onOpenDrawer
                )
            }

            composable("livePlayer", enterTransition = { EnterTransition.None }, exitTransition = { ExitTransition.None }) {
                SideEffect {
                    activity?.requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE
                }
                DisposableEffect(Unit) {
                    onDispose {
                        activity?.requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_UNSPECIFIED
                        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
                    }
                }
                LiveTvPlayerScreen(
                    playerViewModel = playerViewModel,
                    onBack = {
                        navController.popBackStack()
                    }
                )
            }

            composable(Routes.Movies.route) {
                MoviesScreen(navController, sharedCatalogViewModel, onOpenDrawer)
            }
            composable(Routes.Series.route) {
                SeriesScreen(navController, sharedCatalogViewModel, onOpenDrawer)
            }
            composable(MY_LIST_ROUTE) {
                CatalogScreen(
                    listName = "Mi lista",
                    contentTypeFilter = listOf(
                        M3uContentType.MOVIE,
                        M3uContentType.SERIES,
                        M3uContentType.NOVELA,
                        M3uContentType.ANIME
                    ),
                    viewModel = sharedCatalogViewModel,
                    navController = navController,
                    onBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                    onOpenDrawer = onOpenDrawer
                )
            }
            composable(Routes.Profile.route) { ProfileScreen(navController = navController) }
        }
    }

    val sideBarFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }

    if (isTvDevice) {
        // ✅ MODO TV: barra lateral fija + NavHost
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(Color(0xFF101828))
                // 📺 Tecla MENU del control -> salta directo al menú lateral
                .onPreviewKeyEvent { event ->
                    val native = event.nativeKeyEvent
                    if (native.action == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN &&
                        native.keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MENU
                    ) {
                        runCatching { sideBarFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
                        true
                    } else {
                        false
                    }
                }
        ) {
            // 1. La barra lateral ocupa su propio espacio (108.dp), con margen para el foco.
            if (showTvSideBar) {
                TvSideBar(
                    currentRoute = currentRoute,
                    onNavigate = onNavigate,
                    firstItemFocusRequester = sideBarFocusRequester
                )
            }

            // 2. El contenido ocupa el resto de la pantalla
            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).fillMaxHeight()) {
                navHostContent()
            }
        }
    } else {
        // ✅ MODO CELULAR: drawer normal con swipe
        ModalNavigationDrawer(
            drawerState = drawerState,
            gesturesEnabled = allowDrawerGesture,
            drawerContent = {
                AppDrawerContent(currentRoute = currentRoute, onNavigate = onNavigate)
            },
            content = { navHostContent() }
        )
    }
}

/**
 * Barra lateral fija para Android TV.
 * Navegable con D-pad (arriba/abajo + OK).
 */
@Composable
private fun TvSideBar(
    currentRoute: String?,
    onNavigate: (String) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    firstItemFocusRequester: FocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
) {
    val isLive = currentRoute == Routes.LiveTv.route || currentRoute == "livePlayer"
    val isMovies = currentRoute == Routes.Movies.route || currentRoute?.startsWith("movieDetail") == true || currentRoute?.startsWith("m3u_movie_detail") == true
    val isSeries = currentRoute == Routes.Series.route || currentRoute?.startsWith("seriesDetail") == true || currentRoute?.startsWith("m3u_series_detail") == true
    val isMyList = currentRoute == MY_LIST_ROUTE
    val isProfile = currentRoute == Routes.Profile.route

    val drawerBg = Color(0xEE0B1220)
    val selectedColor = Color(0xFF36D760)
    val white = Color.White

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .width(108.dp)
            .fillMaxHeight()
            .background(drawerBg)
            .padding(vertical = 18.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp, Alignment.CenterVertically)
    ) {
        TvSideBarItem(
            icon = Icons.Default.Tv,
            label = "LIVE",
            selected = isLive,
            selectedColor = selectedColor,
            contentColor = white,
            onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.LiveTv.route) },
            modifier = if (isLive) Modifier.focusRequester(firstItemFocusRequester) else Modifier
        )
        TvSideBarItem(
            icon = Icons.Default.Movie,
            label = "Películas",
            selected = isMovies,
            selectedColor = selectedColor,
            contentColor = white,
            onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.Movies.route) },
            modifier = if (isMovies) Modifier.focusRequester(firstItemFocusRequester) else Modifier
        )
        TvSideBarItem(
            icon = Icons.Default.VideoLibrary,
            label = "Series",
            selected = isSeries,
            selectedColor = selectedColor,
            contentColor = white,
            onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.Series.route) },
            modifier = if (isSeries) Modifier.focusRequester(firstItemFocusRequester) else Modifier
        )
        TvSideBarItem(
            icon = Icons.Default.Bookmark,
            label = "Mi lista",
            selected = isMyList,
            selectedColor = selectedColor,
            contentColor = white,
            onClick = { onNavigate(MY_LIST_ROUTE) },
            modifier = if (isMyList) Modifier.focusRequester(firstItemFocusRequester) else Modifier
        )
        TvSideBarItem(
            icon = Icons.Default.Person,
            label = "Perfil",
            selected = isProfile,
            selectedColor = selectedColor,
            contentColor = white,
            onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.Profile.route) },
            modifier = if (isProfile) Modifier.focusRequester(firstItemFocusRequester) else Modifier
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun TvSideBarItem(
    icon: ImageVector,
    label: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    selectedColor: Color,
    contentColor: Color,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    var isFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val itemShape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isFocused) 1.045f else 1f,
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
        label = "sideBarScale"
    )
    val tint = when {
        isFocused -> Color.White
        selected -> selectedColor
        else -> contentColor
    }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 5.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
            .graphicsLayer { scaleX = scale; scaleY = scale }
            .zIndex(if (isFocused) 1f else 0f)
            .clip(itemShape)
            .background(
                when {
                    isFocused -> Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f)
                    selected -> selectedColor.copy(alpha = 0.12f)
                    else -> Color.Transparent
                }
            )
            // ✅ ANTES del clickable y con hasFocus
            .onFocusChanged { isFocused = it.hasFocus }
            // ✅ UN solo foco, y este SÍ tiene onClick -> OK funciona
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = null,
                onClick = onClick
            )
            .padding(vertical = 8.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = label,
            tint = tint,
            modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
        Text(
            text = label,
            color = tint,
            fontSize = 11.sp,
            fontWeight = if (selected || isFocused) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
            maxLines = 1,
            softWrap = false
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AppDrawerContent(currentRoute: String?, onNavigate: (String) -> Unit) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val isLive = currentRoute == Routes.LiveTv.route || currentRoute == "livePlayer"
    val isMovies = currentRoute == Routes.Movies.route || currentRoute?.startsWith("movieDetail") == true || currentRoute?.startsWith("m3u_movie_detail") == true
    val isSeries = currentRoute == Routes.Series.route || currentRoute?.startsWith("seriesDetail") == true || currentRoute?.startsWith("m3u_series_detail") == true
    val isMyList = currentRoute == MY_LIST_ROUTE
    val isProfile = currentRoute == Routes.Profile.route

    val drawerBg = Color(0xFF0B1220)
    val drawerSurface = Color(0xFF151E30)
    val chipSelected = Color(0xFF36D760)
    val white = Color.White
    val softText = Color(0xFFB0B8C5)

    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxHeight().width(280.dp).background(drawerBg)) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(160.dp).background(drawerSurface), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                    Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Star, contentDescription = "Logo", tint = chipSelected, modifier = Modifier.size(56.dp))
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    Text(text = "Latino IPTV Pro", color = white, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
            DrawerMenuItem(icon = Icons.Default.Tv, text = "LIVE", selected = isLive, onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.LiveTv.route) })
            DrawerMenuItem(icon = Icons.Default.Movie, text = "Películas", selected = isMovies, onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.Movies.route) })
            DrawerMenuItem(icon = Icons.Default.VideoLibrary, text = "Series", selected = isSeries, onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.Series.route) })
            DrawerMenuItem(icon = Icons.Default.Bookmark, text = "Mi lista", selected = isMyList, onClick = { onNavigate(MY_LIST_ROUTE) })
            DrawerMenuItem(icon = Icons.Default.Person, text = "Perfil", selected = isProfile, onClick = { onNavigate(Routes.Profile.route) })
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
            HorizontalDivider(color = Color(0xFF2B2F3A), thickness = 1.dp, modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp))
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
            Text(text = "Agrupar por", color = softText, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp))
            DrawerMenuItem(icon = Icons.Default.Category, text = "Categorías", selected = false, onClick = { scope.launch { DrawerActions.onSetGroupingMode.emit(LiveCatalogViewModel.GroupingMode.CATEGORY) }; onNavigate(Routes.LiveTv.route) })
            DrawerMenuItem(icon = Icons.Default.Public, text = "Países", selected = false, onClick = { scope.launch { DrawerActions.onSetGroupingMode.emit(LiveCatalogViewModel.GroupingMode.COUNTRY) }; onNavigate(Routes.LiveTv.route) })
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
            Text(text = "v1.0.0", color = softText, fontSize = 12.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp).align(Alignment.CenterHorizontally))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DrawerMenuItem(icon: ImageVector, text: String, selected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable(onClick = onClick).padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Icon(imageVector = icon, contentDescription = text, tint = if (selected) Color(0xFF36D760) else Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
        Text(text = text, color = if (selected) Color(0xFF36D760) else Color.White, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium)
    }
}

    var isDetailVisible by mutableStateOf(false)
