@Composable
private fun Modifier.tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice: Boolean): Modifier {
    var isFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isTvDevice && isFocused) 1.02f else 1f,
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
        label = "catalogControlFocusZoom"
    )
    return this
        .onFocusChanged { isFocused = it.isFocused }
        .graphicsLayer { scaleX = scale; scaleY = scale }
        .zIndex(if (isFocused) 1f else 0f)
}

@Composable
fun PremiumLoadingEffectM3u(progress: Float, statusText: String) {
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "pulse")
    val scale by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.95f, targetValue = 1.05f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(animation = tween(1000, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing), repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse),
        label = "scale"
    )
    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(M3uBgColor), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
            Text(text = "INICIANDO CONTENIDO", color = Color(0xFFFFD700), fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, modifier = Modifier.scale(scale))
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
            CircularProgressIndicator(color = M3uRedBadge, strokeWidth = 4.dp)
            if (statusText.isNotBlank()) {
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                Text(statusText, color = Color.White, fontSize = 14.sp)
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
            LinearProgressIndicator(progress = { progress }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(0.5f).height(3.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)), color = M3uRedBadge, trackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
private fun UnifiedPosterGrid(
    items: List<GridItemData>,
    isTvDevice: Boolean,
    firstItemFocusRequester: FocusRequester? = null,
    focusRequesterForItem: ((GridItemData) -> FocusRequester)? = null,
    onPosterFocused: ((FocusRequester) -> Unit)? = null
) {
    LazyVerticalGrid(
        columns = GridCells.Adaptive(minSize = 150.dp),
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        itemsIndexed(
            items = items,
            key = { index, item -> "poster_${index}_${item.title}_${item.yearFallback.orEmpty()}_${item.logo}" }
        ) { index, item ->
            var posterHasFocus by remember(item.title, item.logo) { mutableStateOf(false) }
            val cardFocusRequester = when {
                index == 0 && firstItemFocusRequester != null -> firstItemFocusRequester
                focusRequesterForItem != null -> focusRequesterForItem(item)
                else -> null
            }
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().zIndex(if (posterHasFocus) 1f else 0f),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.TopCenter
            ) {
                FocusZoomPosterCard(
                    item = item,
                    isTvDevice = isTvDevice,
                    modifier = Modifier.width(if (isTvDevice) 135.dp else 150.dp).then(
                        if (cardFocusRequester != null) Modifier.focusRequester(cardFocusRequester) else Modifier
                    ),
                    onFocusChanged = { focused ->
                        posterHasFocus = focused
                        if (focused && cardFocusRequester != null) onPosterFocused?.invoke(cardFocusRequester)
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun FocusZoomPosterCard(
    item: GridItemData,
    isTvDevice: Boolean,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onFocusChanged: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}
) {
    var isFocused by remember(item.title, item.logo) { mutableStateOf(false) }

    NetflixPosterCard(
        item = item,
        modifier = modifier
            .width(if (isTvDevice) 135.dp else 150.dp)
            .onFocusChanged {
                isFocused = it.isFocused
                onFocusChanged(it.isFocused)
            }
            // En TV indicamos el foco con un borde, sin agrandar el póster.
            .then(
                if (isTvDevice && isFocused) {
                    Modifier.border(3.dp, Color.White, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                } else {
                    Modifier
                }
            )
            .zIndex(if (isFocused) 1f else 0f)
    )
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class, ExperimentalFoundationApi::class, ExperimentalMaterialApi::class)
@Composable
fun CatalogScreen(
    listName: String,
    contentTypeFilter: List<M3uContentType>,
    viewModel: SharedCatalogViewModel,
    navController: NavController,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onOpenDrawer: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val isTvDevice = remember(context) { context.isTv() }
    val isMyListScreen = listName.equals("Mi lista", ignoreCase = true)
    val movies by viewModel.moviesList.collectAsState()
    val seriesMap by viewModel.seriesMap.collectAsState()
    val sagasMap by viewModel.sagasMap.collectAsState()
    val genresMap by viewModel.genresMap.collectAsState()
    val allTopCarousel by viewModel.topCarousel.collectAsState()
    val topCarouselTmdb by viewModel.topCarouselTmdb.collectAsState()
    val recentItems by viewModel.recentItems.collectAsState()
    val recentlyAddedItems by viewModel.recentlyAdded.collectAsState()
    val tmdbInfo by viewModel.tmdbInfo.collectAsState()
    val tmdbLoading by viewModel.tmdbLoading.collectAsState()
    val isLoading by viewModel.isLoading.collectAsState()
    val loadError by viewModel.loadError.collectAsState()
    val searchQuery by viewModel.searchQuery.collectAsState()
    var isSearching by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val isSearchingData by viewModel.isSearchingData.collectAsState()
    val globalSearchResults by viewModel.globalSearchResults.collectAsState()
    val searchFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    var selectedMovie by remember { mutableStateOf<M3uChannel?>(null) }
    var selectedSeries by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var selectedSaga by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    SideEffect {
        M3uCatalogNavigationState.isDetailVisible =
            selectedMovie != null || selectedSeries != null || selectedSaga != null
    }
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose { M3uCatalogNavigationState.isDetailVisible = false }
    }
    var viewingCategory by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var myListItems by remember { mutableStateOf(M3uMyListStore.load(context)) }
    val firstPosterFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val rowPosterFocusRequesters = remember { mutableMapOf<String, FocusRequester>() }
    val gridPosterFocusRequesters = remember { mutableMapOf<String, FocusRequester>() }
    val resumeFocusRequesters = remember { mutableMapOf<String, FocusRequester>() }
    val carouselFocusRequesters = remember { mutableMapOf<String, FocusRequester>() }
    var lastFocusedPosterRequester by remember { mutableStateOf<FocusRequester?>(null) }
    var initialScreenFocusHandled by remember(listName) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var pendingVerMasFocusKey by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var hadDetailOpen by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    fun gridFocusKey(item: GridItemData): String = "${item.title}|${item.yearFallback.orEmpty()}|${item.logo}"
    fun gridFocusRequester(item: GridItemData): FocusRequester =
        gridPosterFocusRequesters.getOrPut(gridFocusKey(item)) { FocusRequester() }
    LaunchedEffect(listName) {
        viewModel.loadData()
        isSearching = false
        viewModel.updateSearch("")
    }
    LaunchedEffect(movies.size, seriesMap.size, sagasMap.size) {
        if (movies.isNotEmpty() || seriesMap.isNotEmpty() || sagasMap.isNotEmpty()) {
            viewModel.refreshHistory()
        }
    }
    val listId = viewModel.getActiveListId()
    val filteredMovies = movies.filter { contentTypeFilter.contains(it.contentType) }
    val filteredSeries = seriesMap.filterKeys { seriesName -> seriesMap[seriesName]?.any { it.contentType in contentTypeFilter } == true }
    val filteredSagas = sagasMap.filterKeys { sagaName -> sagasMap[sagaName]?.any { it.contentType in contentTypeFilter } == true }
    val topCarousel = allTopCarousel.filter { contentTypeFilter.contains(it.contentType) }
    val filteredRecentlyAdded = recentlyAddedItems.filter { contentTypeFilter.contains(it.contentType) }
    val filteredMyListItems = myListItems.filter { saved ->
        when (saved.mediaType) {
            M3uMyListEntry.MOVIE -> contentTypeFilter.contains(M3uContentType.MOVIE)
            M3uMyListEntry.SERIES -> contentTypeFilter.any {
                it == M3uContentType.SERIES || it == M3uContentType.NOVELA || it == M3uContentType.ANIME
            }
            else -> false
        }
    }.asReversed()
    val allowedWatchTypes = contentTypeFilter.mapNotNull {
        when (it) {
            M3uContentType.MOVIE -> M3uWatchType.MOVIE
            M3uContentType.SAGA -> M3uWatchType.SAGA
            M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> M3uWatchType.SERIES
            else -> null
        }
    }
    val filteredRecentItems = recentItems.filter { allowedWatchTypes.contains(it.entry.watchType) }

    // 📺 Orden real de las filas del inicio y sus ítems, para cablear el foco D-pad.
    val catalogRows = remember(filteredRecentlyAdded, filteredMovies, filteredSeries, filteredSagas, genresMap, contentTypeFilter) {
        val rowKeyOf: (M3uChannel) -> String = { ch ->
            if (ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SERIES || ch.contentType == M3uContentType.NOVELA ||
                ch.contentType == M3uContentType.ANIME || ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SAGA
            ) "${ch.contentType}_${ch.rootName}" else ch.dedupeKey
        }
        val cats = listOf("Películas", "Series", "Sagas").filter { cat ->
            when (cat) {
                "Películas" -> filteredMovies.isNotEmpty() && contentTypeFilter.contains(M3uContentType.MOVIE)
                "Series" -> filteredSeries.isNotEmpty()
                "Sagas" -> filteredSagas.isNotEmpty()
                else -> false
            }
        } + genresMap.keys.sorted().filter { genre ->
            genresMap[genre]?.any { it.contentType in contentTypeFilter } == true
        }
        val order = mutableListOf<String>()
        val itemsByKey = mutableMapOf<String, List<M3uChannel>>()
        if (filteredRecentlyAdded.isNotEmpty()) {
            order.add("Recién Agregados")
            itemsByKey["Recién Agregados"] = filteredRecentlyAdded.take(15).distinctBy(rowKeyOf)
        }
        cats.forEach { cat ->
            val source = when (cat) {
                "Películas" -> filteredMovies
                "Series" -> filteredSeries.values.mapNotNull { it.firstOrNull() }
                "Sagas" -> filteredSagas.values.mapNotNull { it.firstOrNull() }
                else -> (genresMap[cat] ?: emptyList()).filter { it.contentType in contentTypeFilter }
            }
            val row = source.take(15).distinctBy(rowKeyOf)
            if (row.isNotEmpty()) {
                order.add(cat)
                itemsByKey[cat] = row
            }
        }
        order to itemsByKey
    }
    val rowOrder = catalogRows.first
    val rowItemsByKey = catalogRows.second
    val verMasRequesters = remember(rowOrder) { rowOrder.associateWith { FocusRequester() } }
    val firstPosterRequesters = remember(rowOrder) { rowOrder.associateWith { FocusRequester() } }

    val listState = rememberLazyListState()
    val catalogScope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    fun rowFocusModifier(rowKey: String, ch: M3uChannel, rowItems: List<M3uChannel>): Modifier {
        if (rowItems.isEmpty()) return Modifier
        val idx = rowOrder.indexOf(rowKey)
        val isFirst = ch.url == rowItems.first().url
        val isLast = ch.url == rowItems.last().url
        val prevKey = rowOrder.getOrNull(idx - 1)
        val nextKey = rowOrder.getOrNull(idx + 1)
        val baseIndex = (if (topCarousel.isNotEmpty()) 1 else 0) + (if (filteredRecentItems.isNotEmpty()) 1 else 0)
        val requesterKey = "$rowKey|${ch.url}"
        val cardRequester = rowPosterFocusRequesters.getOrPut(requesterKey) {
            if (isFirst) firstPosterRequesters[rowKey]!! else FocusRequester()
        }
        return Modifier
            .focusRequester(cardRequester)
            .onFocusChanged { if (it.isFocused) lastFocusedPosterRequester = cardRequester }
            .focusProperties {
                if (isLast) {
                    // Desde el último póster, derecha lleva a "Ver más".
                    right = verMasRequesters[rowKey]!!

                    // Conserva también el acceso hacia arriba al encabezado.
                    up = verMasRequesters[rowKey]!!
                }
            }
            .onKeyEvent { event ->
                if (event.type != KeyEventType.KeyDown) return@onKeyEvent false
                val targetKey = when (event.key) {
                    Key.DirectionDown -> if (isLast) nextKey else null
                    Key.DirectionUp -> if (isFirst) prevKey else null
                    else -> null
                } ?: return@onKeyEvent false
                // Saltos entre filas: primero hacemos scroll (la fila se compone) y LUEGO pedimos foco.
                val targetIndex = baseIndex + rowOrder.indexOf(targetKey)
                catalogScope.launch {
                    listState.animateScrollToItem(targetIndex)
                    runCatching { firstPosterRequesters[targetKey]?.requestFocus() }
                }
                true
            }
    }

    var showRefreshDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showRefreshProgressDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var refreshSyncStarted by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var cancelButtonFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var updateButtonFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var pendingRefresh by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val cancelButtonFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val updateButtonFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val pullRefreshState = rememberPullRefreshState(
        refreshing = isLoading,
        onRefresh = { showRefreshDialog = true }
    )
    val pagerState = rememberPagerState(pageCount = { topCarousel.size })
    LaunchedEffect(
        listName,
        topCarousel.size,
        filteredRecentItems.size,
        rowOrder.size,
        isLoading
    ) {
        if (!isTvDevice || initialScreenFocusHandled || isLoading) {
            return@LaunchedEffect
        }

        val isSeriesScreen = listName.equals("Series", ignoreCase = true)
        val firstRecentItem = filteredRecentItems.firstOrNull()

        when {
            isSeriesScreen && firstRecentItem != null -> {
                val recentFocusKey =
                    "cw_${firstRecentItem.entry.watchType}_${firstRecentItem.movie?.dedupeKey ?: firstRecentItem.sagaMovie?.dedupeKey ?: (firstRecentItem.seriesName + "_" + firstRecentItem.entry.contentUrl)}"

                val continueWatchingIndex = if (topCarousel.isNotEmpty()) 1 else 0
                listState.scrollToItem(continueWatchingIndex)
                delay(200)

                val requester = resumeFocusRequesters.getOrPut(recentFocusKey) {
                    FocusRequester()
                }

                runCatching { requester.requestFocus() }
                    .onSuccess { initialScreenFocusHandled = true }
            }

            topCarousel.isNotEmpty() -> {
                listState.scrollToItem(0)
                pagerState.scrollToPage(0)
                delay(200)

                val firstCarouselItem = topCarousel.first()
                val requester = carouselFocusRequesters.getOrPut(firstCarouselItem.url) {
                    FocusRequester()
                }

                runCatching { requester.requestFocus() }
                    .onSuccess { initialScreenFocusHandled = true }
            }

            firstRecentItem != null -> {
                val recentFocusKey =
                    "cw_${firstRecentItem.entry.watchType}_${firstRecentItem.movie?.dedupeKey ?: firstRecentItem.sagaMovie?.dedupeKey ?: (firstRecentItem.seriesName + "_" + firstRecentItem.entry.contentUrl)}"

                listState.scrollToItem(0)
                delay(200)

                val requester = resumeFocusRequesters.getOrPut(recentFocusKey) {
                    FocusRequester()
                }

                runCatching { requester.requestFocus() }
                    .onSuccess { initialScreenFocusHandled = true }
            }

            else -> {
                val firstRow = rowOrder.firstOrNull() ?: return@LaunchedEffect
                val firstRowIndex =
                    (if (topCarousel.isNotEmpty()) 1 else 0) +
                            (if (filteredRecentItems.isNotEmpty()) 1 else 0)

                listState.scrollToItem(firstRowIndex)
                delay(200)

                val requester = firstPosterRequesters[firstRow]
                    ?: return@LaunchedEffect

                runCatching { requester.requestFocus() }
                    .onSuccess { initialScreenFocusHandled = true }
            }
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(pagerState, topCarousel.size) {
        if (topCarousel.isNotEmpty()) {
            while (true) {
                delay(6000)
                val nextPage = (pagerState.currentPage + 1) % topCarousel.size
                pagerState.animateScrollToPage(page = nextPage, animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 1500, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing))
            }
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(isSearching) {
        if (isSearching) { delay(50); runCatching { searchFocusRequester.requestFocus() } }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(showRefreshDialog) {
        if (showRefreshDialog) {
            delay(150)
            runCatching { updateButtonFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(pendingRefresh) {
        if (pendingRefresh) {
            viewModel.forceSync()
            pendingRefresh = false
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(showRefreshProgressDialog, isLoading, refreshSyncStarted) {
        if (!showRefreshProgressDialog) return@LaunchedEffect

        if (isLoading) {
            refreshSyncStarted = true
        } else if (refreshSyncStarted) {
            refreshSyncStarted = false
            showRefreshProgressDialog = false
        } else {
            // Evita dejar abierto el popup si la sincronización no llega a iniciarse.
            delay(5000)
            if (showRefreshProgressDialog && !isLoading && !refreshSyncStarted) {
                showRefreshProgressDialog = false
            }
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(viewingCategory) {
        if (viewingCategory != null) {
            delay(350)
            runCatching { firstPosterFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(selectedMovie, selectedSeries, selectedSaga) {
        val detailIsOpen = selectedMovie != null || selectedSeries != null || selectedSaga != null
        if (detailIsOpen) {
            hadDetailOpen = true
        } else if (hadDetailOpen) {
            hadDetailOpen = false
            delay(350)
            runCatching { lastFocusedPosterRequester?.requestFocus() }
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(viewingCategory, pendingVerMasFocusKey) {
        val key = pendingVerMasFocusKey
        if (viewingCategory == null && key != null) {
            delay(250)
            runCatching { verMasRequesters[key]?.requestFocus() }
            pendingVerMasFocusKey = null
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(isMyListScreen, filteredMyListItems.size) {
        if (isMyListScreen && filteredMyListItems.isNotEmpty()) {
            delay(350)
            runCatching { firstPosterFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
        }
    }
    BackHandler(enabled = viewingCategory != null && selectedMovie == null && selectedSeries == null && selectedSaga == null) {
        pendingVerMasFocusKey = viewingCategory
        viewingCategory = null
    }
    val hasContent = filteredMovies.isNotEmpty() || filteredSeries.isNotEmpty() || filteredSagas.isNotEmpty()
    var showDeleteDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var deleteCandidate by remember { mutableStateOf<M3uRecentUiItem?>(null) }
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(M3uBgColor)
            .padding(WindowInsets.systemBars.asPaddingValues())
    ) {
        when {
            selectedMovie != null && selectedSeries == null && selectedSaga == null -> {
                key(selectedMovie!!.url) {
                    M3uMovieDetailScreen(movie = selectedMovie!!, listId = listId, tmdbInfo = tmdbInfo, tmdbLoading = tmdbLoading, allMovies = filteredMovies, onMovieClick = { selectedMovie = it; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(it.title, true, it.year) }, onBack = { selectedMovie = null; viewModel.refreshHistory() }, onMyListChanged = { myListItems = M3uMyListStore.load(context) })
                }
            }
            selectedSaga != null -> {
                M3uSagaDetailScreen(sagaName = selectedSaga!!, listId = listId, sagaMovies = sagasMap[selectedSaga] ?: emptyList(), tmdbInfo = tmdbInfo, tmdbLoading = tmdbLoading, onBack = { selectedSaga = null; viewModel.refreshHistory() })
            }
            selectedSeries != null -> {
                key(selectedSeries!!) {
                    M3uSeriesDetailScreen(
                        seriesName = selectedSeries!!,
                        listId = listId,
                        episodesFlatList = seriesMap[selectedSeries] ?: emptyList(),
                        tmdbInfo = tmdbInfo,
                        tmdbLoading = tmdbLoading,
                        onBack = { selectedSeries = null; viewModel.refreshHistory() },
                        onMyListChanged = { myListItems = M3uMyListStore.load(context) }
                    )
                }
            }
            isMyListScreen -> {
                val savedGridItems = filteredMyListItems.map { saved ->
                    val savedMovie = if (saved.mediaType == M3uMyListEntry.MOVIE) {
                        movies.firstOrNull { it.url == saved.mediaId }
                    } else null
                    val savedSeriesName = if (saved.mediaType == M3uMyListEntry.SERIES) {
                        seriesMap.keys.firstOrNull { it.equals(saved.mediaId, ignoreCase = true) }
                    } else null
                    val savedEpisode = savedSeriesName?.let { seriesMap[it]?.firstOrNull() }
                    val image = saved.logo.ifBlank { savedMovie?.logo ?: savedEpisode?.logo.orEmpty() }
                    val cardYear = saved.year.ifBlank { savedMovie?.year ?: savedEpisode?.year.orEmpty() }
                    val cardLanguage = saved.language.ifBlank { savedMovie?.language ?: savedEpisode?.language.orEmpty() }
                    val cardQuality = saved.quality.ifBlank { savedMovie?.quality ?: savedEpisode?.quality.orEmpty() }
                    GridItemData(
                        title = saved.title,
                        logo = image,
                        language = cardLanguage,
                        quality = cardQuality,
                        isEmbed = savedMovie?.isEmbed ?: savedEpisode?.isEmbed ?: false,
                        yearFallback = cardYear
                    ) {
                        when (saved.mediaType) {
                            M3uMyListEntry.MOVIE -> savedMovie?.let { movie ->
                                selectedMovie = movie
                                viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(movie.title, true, movie.year)
                            }
                            M3uMyListEntry.SERIES -> {
                                val name = savedSeriesName
                                val firstEpisode = savedEpisode
                                if (name != null && firstEpisode != null) {
                                    if (WorkerResolver.isSeriesWorkerUrl(firstEpisode.url)) {
                                        navController.navigate("series_detail/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(name, "UTF-8")}/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(firstEpisode.url, "UTF-8")}")
                                    } else {
                                        selectedSeries = name
                                        viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(name, false, firstEpisode.year)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    Text(
                        text = "MI LISTA",
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontSize = 20.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 16.dp)
                    )
                    if (savedGridItems.isEmpty()) {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally, modifier = Modifier.padding(32.dp)) {
                                Icon(Icons.Default.Bookmark, contentDescription = null, tint = M3uSoftText, modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp))
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                                Text("Tu lista está vacía", color = Color.White, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                Text("Agrega películas o series desde su pantalla de detalles.", color = M3uSoftText, fontSize = 14.sp, textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        UnifiedPosterGrid(
                            items = savedGridItems,
                            isTvDevice = isTvDevice,
                            firstItemFocusRequester = firstPosterFocusRequester,
                            focusRequesterForItem = { item -> gridFocusRequester(item) },
                            onPosterFocused = { lastFocusedPosterRequester = it }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            viewingCategory != null -> {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(M3uCardColor).padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 4.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        IconButton(onClick = { pendingVerMasFocusKey = viewingCategory; viewingCategory = null }, modifier = Modifier.tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)) { Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack, null, tint = Color.White) }
                        Text(text = viewingCategory!!, color = Color.White, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, maxLines = 1, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                    }
                    val gridItems = remember(viewingCategory, filteredSeries, filteredSagas, filteredMovies, genresMap, filteredRecentlyAdded) {
                        when (viewingCategory) {
                            "Recién Agregados" -> filteredRecentlyAdded.map { ch ->
                                val finalTitle = if (ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SERIES || ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SAGA) ch.rootName else ch.cleanTitle
                                val tempsOrPelis = when(ch.contentType) {
                                    M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> "${seriesMap[ch.rootName].orEmpty().map { it.group }.distinct().size} Temp."
                                    M3uContentType.SAGA -> "${sagasMap[ch.rootName].orEmpty().size} pelis"
                                    else -> null
                                }
                                GridItemData(title = finalTitle, logo = ch.logo, badgeText = tempsOrPelis ?: "Nuevo", language = ch.language, quality = ch.quality, isEmbed = ch.isEmbed, yearFallback = ch.year ?: "") {
                                    when (ch.contentType) {
                                        M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> {
                                            selectedSeries = ch.rootName
                                            viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.rootName, false, ch.year)
                                        }
                                        M3uContentType.SAGA -> { selectedSaga = ch.rootName; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.rootName, true, ch.year) }
                                        else -> { selectedMovie = ch; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.title, true, ch.year) }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            "Sagas" -> filteredSagas.map { (name, movies) -> val f = movies.firstOrNull(); GridItemData(title = name, logo = f?.logo ?: "", badgeText = "${movies.size} pelis", language = f?.language, quality = f?.quality, isEmbed = f?.isEmbed ?: false, yearFallback = f?.year ?: "") { selectedSaga = name; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(f?.title ?: "", true, f?.year) } }
                            "Películas" -> filteredMovies.map { movie -> GridItemData(title = movie.cleanTitle, logo = movie.logo, language = movie.language, quality = movie.quality, isEmbed = movie.isEmbed, yearFallback = movie.year ?: "") { selectedMovie = movie; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(movie.title, true, movie.year) } }
                            "Series" -> filteredSeries.map { (name, eps) -> val f = eps.firstOrNull(); val temps = eps.map { it.group }.distinct().size; GridItemData(title = name, logo = f?.logo ?: "", badgeText = "$temps Temp.", language = f?.language, quality = f?.quality, isEmbed = f?.isEmbed ?: false, yearFallback = f?.year ?: "") {
                                if (WorkerResolver.isSeriesWorkerUrl(f?.url ?: "")) {
                                    navController.navigate("series_detail/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(name, "UTF-8")}/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(f?.url ?: "", "UTF-8")}")
                                } else {
                                    selectedSeries = name; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(name, false, f?.year)
                                }
                            } }
                            else -> {
                                (genresMap[viewingCategory] ?: emptyList()).filter { contentTypeFilter.contains(it.contentType) }.map { channel ->
                                    val titleStr = if (channel.contentType == M3uContentType.SERIES || channel.contentType == M3uContentType.NOVELA || channel.contentType == M3uContentType.ANIME || channel.contentType == M3uContentType.SAGA) channel.rootName else channel.cleanTitle
                                    val tempsOrPelis = when(channel.contentType) {
                                        M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> "${seriesMap[channel.rootName].orEmpty().map { it.group }.distinct().size} Temp."
                                        M3uContentType.SAGA -> "${sagasMap[channel.rootName].orEmpty().size} pelis"
                                        else -> null
                                    }
                                    GridItemData(title = titleStr, logo = channel.logo, badgeText = tempsOrPelis, language = channel.language, quality = channel.quality, isEmbed = channel.isEmbed, yearFallback = channel.year ?: "") {
                                        when (channel.contentType) {
                                            M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> {
                                                if (WorkerResolver.isSeriesWorkerUrl(channel.url)) {
                                                    navController.navigate("series_detail/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(channel.rootName, "UTF-8")}/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(channel.url, "UTF-8")}")
                                                } else {
                                                    selectedSeries = channel.rootName; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(channel.rootName, false, channel.year)
                                                }
                                            }
                                            M3uContentType.SAGA -> { selectedSaga = channel.rootName; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(channel.rootName, true, channel.year) }
                                            else -> { selectedMovie = channel; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(channel.title, true, channel.year) }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Box(modifier = if (isTvDevice) Modifier.fillMaxSize() else Modifier.fillMaxSize().pullRefresh(pullRefreshState)) {
                        UnifiedPosterGrid(
                            items = gridItems,
                            isTvDevice = isTvDevice,
                            firstItemFocusRequester = firstPosterFocusRequester,
                            focusRequesterForItem = { item -> gridFocusRequester(item) },
                            onPosterFocused = { lastFocusedPosterRequester = it }
                        )
                        if (isTvDevice) {
                            if (isLoading) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .align(Alignment.TopCenter)
                                        .padding(top = 16.dp)
                                        .zIndex(2f)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                                        .background(Color(0xFF1B2636))
                                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 10.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        CircularProgressIndicator(
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp),
                                            color = M3uRedBadge,
                                            strokeWidth = 2.dp
                                        )
                                        Text(
                                            text = "Actualizando catálogo...",
                                            color = Color.White,
                                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            PullRefreshIndicator(
                                refreshing = isLoading,
                                state = pullRefreshState,
                                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter),
                                backgroundColor = Color(0xFF1B2636),
                                contentColor = M3uRedBadge
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            else -> {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(Color.Transparent).padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 4.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        if (isSearching) {
                            IconButton(onClick = { isSearching = false; viewModel.updateSearch("") }, modifier = Modifier.tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)) { Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack, null, tint = Color.White) }
                            TextField(value = searchQuery, onValueChange = { viewModel.updateSearch(it) }, placeholder = { Text("Buscar...", color = M3uSoftText) }, singleLine = true, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).focusRequester(searchFocusRequester), colors = TextFieldDefaults.colors(focusedContainerColor = Color.Transparent, unfocusedContainerColor = Color.Transparent, focusedTextColor = Color.White, unfocusedTextColor = Color.White, focusedIndicatorColor = Color.Transparent, unfocusedIndicatorColor = Color.Transparent))
                            if (searchQuery.isNotEmpty()) { IconButton(onClick = { viewModel.updateSearch("") }, modifier = Modifier.tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)) { Icon(Icons.Default.Close, null, tint = Color.White) } }
                        } else {
                            IconButton(onClick = onOpenDrawer, modifier = Modifier.tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)) {
                                Icon(Icons.Default.Menu, contentDescription = "Menú", tint = Color.White)
                            }
                            if (isTvDevice) {
                                Text(text = listName, color = Color.White, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                                IconButton(onClick = { showRefreshDialog = true }, modifier = Modifier.tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Default.Refresh, contentDescription = "Actualizar $listName", tint = Color.White)
                                }
                                Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                            } else {
                                Text(text = listName, color = Color.White, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                            }
                            IconButton(onClick = { isSearching = true }, modifier = Modifier.tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)) { Icon(Icons.Default.Search, null, tint = Color.White) }
                        }
                    }
                    when {
                        isLoading && !hasContent -> {
                            PremiumLoadingEffectM3u(progress = 0.5f, statusText = "Preparando $listName...")
                        }
                        loadError != null && !hasContent -> Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), Alignment.Center) { Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) { Text("⚠️", fontSize = 40.sp); Text(loadError!!, color = M3uSoftText); Button(onClick = { viewModel.loadData() }) { Text("Reintentar") } } }
                        !hasContent && searchQuery.isBlank() -> Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), Alignment.Center) { Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) { Text("No hay contenido para mostrar", color = Color.White, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold); Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp)); Button(onClick = { viewModel.forceSync() }) { Text("Volver a intentar") } } }
                        else -> {
                            Box(modifier = if (isTvDevice) Modifier.fillMaxSize() else Modifier.fillMaxSize().pullRefresh(pullRefreshState)) {
                                if (searchQuery.isNotBlank()) {
                                    val filteredSearchResults = remember(globalSearchResults, contentTypeFilter) {
                                        globalSearchResults.filter { contentTypeFilter.contains(it.contentType) }
                                    }
                                    if (isSearchingData) {
                                        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) { CircularProgressIndicator(color = M3uRedBadge); Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp)); Text("Buscando...", color = Color.White, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) } }
                                    } else if (filteredSearchResults.isEmpty()) {
                                        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally, modifier = Modifier.padding(24.dp)) {
                                                Text("🔍", fontSize = 48.sp)
                                                Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                                                Text("No se encontraron resultados", color = Color.White, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                                                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                                Text("No encontramos películas o series para \"$searchQuery\". Prueba con otro título.", color = M3uSoftText, fontSize = 14.sp, textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                                            }
                                        }
                                    } else {
                                        val searchItems = remember(filteredSearchResults) {
                                            filteredSearchResults.map { ch ->
                                                val finalTitle = if (ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SERIES || ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SAGA) ch.rootName else ch.cleanTitle
                                                val tempsOrPelis = when(ch.contentType) {
                                                    M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> "${seriesMap[ch.rootName].orEmpty().map { it.group }.distinct().size} Temp."
                                                    M3uContentType.SAGA -> "${sagasMap[ch.rootName].orEmpty().size} pelis"
                                                    else -> null
                                                }
                                                GridItemData(title = finalTitle, logo = ch.logo, badgeText = tempsOrPelis, language = ch.language, quality = ch.quality, isEmbed = ch.isEmbed, yearFallback = ch.year ?: "") {
                                                    when (ch.contentType) {
                                                        M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> { selectedSeries = ch.rootName; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.rootName, false, ch.year) }
                                                        M3uContentType.SAGA -> { selectedSaga = ch.rootName; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.rootName, true, ch.year) }
                                                        else -> { selectedMovie = ch; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.title, true, ch.year) }
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                        UnifiedPosterGrid(
                                            items = searchItems,
                                            isTvDevice = isTvDevice,
                                            firstItemFocusRequester = firstPosterFocusRequester,
                                            focusRequesterForItem = { item -> gridFocusRequester(item) },
                                            onPosterFocused = { lastFocusedPosterRequester = it }
                                        )
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    LazyColumn(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), state = listState) {
                                        // 1. TOP CAROUSEL
                                        if (topCarousel.isNotEmpty()) {
                                            item(key = "top_carousel", contentType = "carousel") {
                                                Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(if (isTvDevice) 350.dp else 450.dp)) {
                                                    HorizontalPager(state = pagerState, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) { page ->
                                                        val m = topCarousel[page]
                                                        val carouselFocusRequester = carouselFocusRequesters.getOrPut(m.url) { FocusRequester() }
                                                        val tmdb = topCarouselTmdb[m.url]
                                                        val imageUrl = if (tmdb?.backdropPath?.isNotEmpty() == true) {
                                                            "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/${if (isTvDevice) "original" else "w1280"}${tmdb.backdropPath}"
                                                        } else m.logo
                                                        val pageOffset = ((pagerState.currentPage - page) + pagerState.currentPageOffsetFraction).absoluteValue
                                                        val scale = 1f - (pageOffset * 0.08f).coerceIn(0f, 0.08f)
                                                        val alpha = 1f - (pageOffset * 0.25f).coerceIn(0f, 0.25f)
                                                        var carouselHasFocus by remember(m.url) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                                                        val carouselFocusScale by animateFloatAsState(
                                                            targetValue = if (isTvDevice && carouselHasFocus) 1.025f else 1f,
                                                            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 200, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                                                            label = "carousel_focus_scale"
                                                        )
                                                        Box(modifier = Modifier
                                                            .fillMaxSize()
                                                            .graphicsLayer { scaleX = scale * carouselFocusScale; scaleY = scale * carouselFocusScale; this.alpha = alpha }
                                                            .then(if (isTvDevice) Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(M3uBgColor) else Modifier)
                                                            .focusRequester(carouselFocusRequester)
                                                            .onFocusChanged {
                                                                carouselHasFocus = it.hasFocus
                                                                if (it.hasFocus) lastFocusedPosterRequester = carouselFocusRequester
                                                            }
                                                            .zIndex(if (carouselHasFocus) 1f else 0f)
                                                            .clickable(
                                                                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                                                                indication = null,
                                                                onClick = { selectedMovie = m; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(m.title, true, m.year) }
                                                            )) {
                                                            AsyncImage(
                                                                model = ImageRequest.Builder(LocalContext.current)
                                                                    .data(imageUrl)
                                                                    .size(if (isTvDevice) 1920 else 1280)
                                                                    .memoryCacheKey("${m.url}_$imageUrl")
                                                                    .crossfade(true)
                                                                    .build(),
                                                                contentDescription = m.cleanTitle,
                                                                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                                                alignment = Alignment.TopCenter,
                                                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                                                            )
                                                            Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.25f)))
                                                            Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Brush.verticalGradient(colors = listOf(Color.Transparent, M3uBgColor.copy(alpha = 0.4f), M3uBgColor.copy(alpha = 0.9f), M3uBgColor), startY = 200f)))
                                                            Column(
                                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                                    .align(if (isTvDevice) Alignment.BottomStart else Alignment.BottomCenter)
                                                                    .padding(start = if (isTvDevice) 40.dp else 24.dp, end = 24.dp, bottom = if (isTvDevice) 34.dp else 40.dp),
                                                                horizontalAlignment = if (isTvDevice) Alignment.Start else Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                                                            ) {
                                                                Text(m.cleanTitle, color = Color.White, fontSize = if (isTvDevice) 21.sp else 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                                                                Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                                                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) { Text(m.genre?.ifBlank { "Tendencia" } ?: "Tendencia", color = M3uSoftText, fontSize = 12.sp) }
                                                                if (!m.year.isNullOrBlank()) { Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp)); Text(text = "Año: ${m.year}", color = M3uSoftText.copy(alpha = 0.7f), fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium) }
                                                                Spacer(Modifier.height(if (isTvDevice) 10.dp else 20.dp))
                                                                Button(onClick = { selectedMovie = m; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(m.title, true, m.year) }, colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color.White, contentColor = Color.Black), shape = RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)) { Icon(Icons.Default.PlayArrow, null); Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp)); Text("Reproducir", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }
                                                            }
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                    Row(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter).padding(bottom = 16.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                                                        repeat(topCarousel.size) { index ->
                                                            val isSelected = pagerState.currentPage == index
                                                            val dotWidth by animateDpAsState(targetValue = if (isSelected) 24.dp else 8.dp, animationSpec = tween(300, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing), label = "widthAnim")
                                                            val dotAlpha by animateFloatAsState(targetValue = if (isSelected) 1f else 0.4f, animationSpec = tween(300), label = "alphaAnim")
                                                            Box(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp).width(dotWidth).clip(CircleShape).background(Color.White.copy(alpha = dotAlpha)))
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                }
                                                Spacer(Modifier.height(if (isTvDevice) 10.dp else 16.dp))
                                            }
                                        }
                                        // 2. CONTINUAR VIENDO
                                        if (filteredRecentItems.isNotEmpty()) {
                                            item(key = "continue_watching", contentType = "continue_watching_header") {
                                                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                                    Text("Continuar viendo", color = Color.White, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp))
                                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                                    LazyRow(contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                                                        items(items = filteredRecentItems.distinctBy { item -> item.movie?.let { "movie_${it.dedupeKey}" } ?: item.sagaMovie?.let { "saga_${it.dedupeKey}" } ?: "series_${item.entry.watchType}_${item.seriesName}_${item.entry.contentUrl}" }, key = { item -> "cw_${item.entry.watchType}_${item.movie?.dedupeKey ?: item.sagaMovie?.dedupeKey ?: (item.seriesName + "_" + item.entry.contentUrl)}" }, contentType = { "recent_item" }) { item ->
                                                            val cwFocusKey = "cw_${item.entry.watchType}_${item.movie?.dedupeKey ?: item.sagaMovie?.dedupeKey ?: (item.seriesName + "_" + item.entry.contentUrl)}"
                                                            val cwFocusRequester = resumeFocusRequesters.getOrPut(cwFocusKey) { FocusRequester() }
                                                            val url = item.movie?.logo ?: item.sagaMovie?.logo ?: item.entry.logoUrl
                                                            val posterYear = item.movie?.year ?: item.sagaMovie?.year
                                                            val posterLanguage = item.movie?.language ?: item.sagaMovie?.language ?: item.episodes.firstOrNull()?.language
                                                            val posterQuality = item.movie?.quality ?: item.sagaMovie?.quality
                                                            val rawTitle = when { item.movie != null -> item.movie.cleanTitle; item.sagaMovie != null -> item.sagaMovie.cleanTitle; item.seriesName.isNotBlank() -> item.seriesName; else -> item.entry.title }
                                                            var showDeleteIcon by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                                                            var cwFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

                                                            Box(
                                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                                    .width(150.dp)
                                                                    .zIndex(if (cwFocused) 1f else 0f)
                                                                    .focusRequester(cwFocusRequester)
                                                                    .onFocusChanged {
                                                                        cwFocused = it.isFocused
                                                                        if (it.isFocused) lastFocusedPosterRequester = cwFocusRequester
                                                                    }
                                                                    .then(
                                                                        if (isTvDevice && cwFocused) {
                                                                            Modifier.border(
                                                                                width = 3.dp,
                                                                                color = Color.White,
                                                                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                                                                            )
                                                                        } else {
                                                                            Modifier
                                                                        }
                                                                    )
                                                                    .combinedClickable(
                                                                    interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                                                                    indication = null,
                                                                    onClick = {
                                                                        if (showDeleteIcon) showDeleteIcon = false else {
                                                                            when (item.entry.watchType) {
                                                                                M3uWatchType.MOVIE -> { item.movie?.let { selectedMovie = it; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(it.title, true, it.year) } }
                                                                                M3uWatchType.SAGA -> { val sn = item.entry.sagaName; val sm = item.sagaMovie; if (sn.isNotBlank() && sm != null && sagasMap.containsKey(sn)) { selectedSaga = sn; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(sm.title, true, sm.year) } }
                                                                                M3uWatchType.SERIES -> {
                                                                                    val rs = item.seriesName
                                                                                    if (rs.isNotBlank() && item.episodes.isNotEmpty()) {
                                                                                        val firstEpUrl = item.episodes.firstOrNull()?.url ?: ""
                                                                                        if (WorkerResolver.isSeriesWorkerUrl(firstEpUrl)) {
                                                                                            navController.navigate("series_detail/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(rs, "UTF-8")}/${java.net.URLEncoder.encode(firstEpUrl, "UTF-8")}")
                                                                                        } else {
                                                                                            selectedSeries = rs; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(rs, false, item.episodes.firstOrNull()?.year)
                                                                                        }
                                                                                    }
                                                                                }
                                                                            }
                                                                        }
                                                                    },
                                                                    onLongClick = { showDeleteIcon = !showDeleteIcon }
                                                                )
                                                            ) {
                                                                Column(modifier = Modifier.width(150.dp)) {
                                                                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().aspectRatio(2f / 3f).clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)).background(M3uCardColor)) {
                                                                        AsyncImage(
                                                                            model = ImageRequest.Builder(LocalContext.current)
                                                                                .data(url)
                                                                                .size(420)
                                                                                .memoryCacheKey("${item.entry.contentUrl}_${url}")
                                                                                .crossfade(false)
                                                                                .build(),
                                                                            contentDescription = null,
                                                                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                                                                        )
                                                                        if (!item.badgeText.isNullOrBlank()) { Box(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopStart).padding(6.dp).background(Color.Black.copy(0.7f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 2.dp)) { Text(item.badgeText, color = Color.White, fontSize = 9.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) } }
                                                                        if (!posterYear.isNullOrBlank() || !posterLanguage.isNullOrBlank() || !posterQuality.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                                                            Row(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter).padding(bottom = if (!item.entry.isFinished && item.entry.progressPercent > 0) 9.dp else 8.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                                                                if (!posterYear.isNullOrBlank()) Text(posterYear, color = Color.White, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.background(Color(0xE6000B18), RoundedCornerShape(50.dp)).padding(horizontal = 9.dp, vertical = 5.dp))
                                                                                if (!posterLanguage.isNullOrBlank()) Text(posterLanguage, color = Color.White, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, maxLines = 1, modifier = Modifier.background(Color(0xE6000B18), RoundedCornerShape(50.dp)).padding(horizontal = 9.dp, vertical = 5.dp))
                                                                                if (!posterQuality.isNullOrBlank()) Text(posterQuality, color = Color.White, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.background(Color(0xE6000B18), RoundedCornerShape(50.dp)).padding(horizontal = 9.dp, vertical = 5.dp))
                                                                            }
                                                                        }
                                                                        if (!item.entry.isFinished && item.entry.progressPercent > 0) { LinearProgressIndicator(progress = { item.entry.progressPercent / 100f }, modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter).fillMaxWidth().height(4.dp), color = M3uRedBadge, trackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f)) }
                                                                    }
                                                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                                                    Text(rawTitle, color = Color.White, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, maxLines = 2, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis, lineHeight = 16.sp, textAlign = TextAlign.Center, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(32.dp))
                                                                }
                                                                if (showDeleteIcon) {
                                                                    Box(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopEnd).offset(x = 8.dp, y = (-8).dp).size(28.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(Color.Red).clickable { M3uWatchHistoryStore.remove(item.entry.contentUrl, item.entry.watchType); viewModel.refreshHistory(); showDeleteIcon = false }, contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { Text("✕", color = Color.White, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }
                                                                }
                                                            }
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                        // 3. RECIÉN AGREGADOS
                                        val recentRowItems = rowItemsByKey["Recién Agregados"].orEmpty()
                                        if (recentRowItems.isNotEmpty()) {
                                            item(key = "recently_added", contentType = "recently_added_header") {
                                                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                                                    Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                                        Column {
                                                            Text("RECIÉN AGREGADOS", color = Color.White, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                                            Text("Lo más nuevo del catálogo", color = M3uSoftText, fontSize = 11.sp)
                                                        }
                                                        var recentlyAddedVerMasFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

                                                        Box(
                                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                                .height(30.dp)
                                                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                                                                .background(
                                                                    brush = Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                                                        listOf(Color(0xFFFF6D00), Color(0xFFD32F2F))
                                                                    )
                                                                )
                                                                .onFocusChanged { recentlyAddedVerMasFocused = it.isFocused }
                                                                .border(
                                                                    width = if (isTvDevice && recentlyAddedVerMasFocused) 3.dp else 0.dp,
                                                                    color = Color.White,
                                                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
                                                                )
                                                                .tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)
                                                                .focusRequester(verMasRequesters["Recién Agregados"]!!)
                                                                .focusProperties {
                                                                    down = firstPosterRequesters["Recién Agregados"]!!
                                                                }
                                                                .clickable(
                                                                    interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                                                                    indication = null,
                                                                    onClick = { viewingCategory = "Recién Agregados" }
                                                                )
                                                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                                                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                                        ) {
                                                            Text("Ver mas", fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                    LazyRow(contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                                                        items(items = recentRowItems, key = { item -> if (item.contentType == M3uContentType.SERIES || item.contentType == M3uContentType.NOVELA || item.contentType == M3uContentType.ANIME || item.contentType == M3uContentType.SAGA) "recent_series_${item.contentType}_${item.rootName}" else "recent_${item.dedupeKey}" }, contentType = { "poster" }) { ch ->
                                                            val finalTitle = if (ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SERIES || ch.contentType == M3uContentType.SAGA) ch.rootName else ch.cleanTitle
                                                            FocusZoomPosterCard(
                                                                item = GridItemData(title = finalTitle, logo = ch.logo, badgeText = "Nuevo", language = ch.language, quality = ch.quality, isEmbed = ch.isEmbed, yearFallback = ch.year ?: "") {
                                                                    when (ch.contentType) {
                                                                        M3uContentType.SERIES, M3uContentType.NOVELA, M3uContentType.ANIME -> { selectedSeries = ch.rootName; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.rootName, false, ch.year) }
                                                                        M3uContentType.SAGA -> { selectedSaga = ch.rootName; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.rootName, true, ch.year) }
                                                                        else -> { selectedMovie = ch; viewModel.loadTmdbInfo(ch.title, true, ch.year) }
                                                                    }
                                                                },
                                                                isTvDevice = isTvDevice,
                                                                modifier = rowFocusModifier("Recién Agregados", ch, recentRowItems)
                                                            )
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                        // 4. RESTO DE GÉNEROS / CATEGORÍAS
                                        rowOrder.filter { it != "Recién Agregados" }.forEach { catName ->
                                            val itemsForCat = rowItemsByKey[catName].orEmpty()
                                            if (itemsForCat.isNotEmpty()) {
                                                item(key = catName + "_section", contentType = "category_section") {
                                                    Column {
                                                        Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {

                                                                    .tvFocusZoom(isTvDevice)

                                                                        down = firstPosterRequesters[catName]!!

                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (showDeleteDialog && deleteCandidate != null) {
            AlertDialog(onDismissRequest = { showDeleteDialog = false; deleteCandidate = null }, title = { Text("Eliminar de Continuar viendo") }, text = { Text("¿Quieres eliminar esta película del historial?") }, confirmButton = { TextButton(onClick = { deleteCandidate?.let { item -> M3uWatchHistoryStore.remove(item.entry.contentUrl, item.entry.watchType); viewModel.refreshHistory() }; showDeleteDialog = false; deleteCandidate = null }) { Text("Eliminar", color = Color.Red) } }, dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { showDeleteDialog = false; deleteCandidate = null }) { Text("Cancelar") } })
        }
    }
}

fun isAudioCodecSupported(mimeType: String): Boolean {
    if (mimeType.isBlank()) return false
    val codecList = MediaCodecList(MediaCodecList.REGULAR_CODECS)
    for (codecInfo in codecList.codecInfos) {
        if (!codecInfo.isEncoder && codecInfo.supportedTypes.contains(mimeType)) {
            return true
        }
    }
    return false
}

sealed class ServerProfile {
    object GoodStream : ServerProfile()
    object Uqload : ServerProfile()
    object SmoothPre : ServerProfile()
    object Callistanise : ServerProfile()
    object Voe : ServerProfile()
    object VidLink : ServerProfile()
    object Odysee : ServerProfile()
    object MinoChinos : ServerProfile()
    object BysejikuartFamily : ServerProfile()
    object Fastream : ServerProfile()
    object Streamtape : ServerProfile()
    object Streamwish : ServerProfile()
    object SaveFiles : ServerProfile()
    object Regionales : ServerProfile()
    object Dailymotion : ServerProfile()
    object Nupload : ServerProfile()
    object Mixdrop : ServerProfile()
    object Vimeos : ServerProfile()
    object Mediafire : ServerProfile()
    object OkRu : ServerProfile()
    object Doodstream : ServerProfile()
    object MovieMaster : ServerProfile()
    object Unknown : ServerProfile()
}

data class ServerPlaybackConfig(
    val sendReferer: Boolean,
    val sendOrigin: Boolean,
    val sendCookies: Boolean,
    val sendSecFetchHeaders: Boolean,
    val useMobileUserAgent: Boolean = false,
    val userAgent: String? = null
)

// 🔑 Deriva las cookies de autorización de Mediafire desde el propio token.
//    URL = /{dkey+firma}/{qkey}/{archivo}  →  cookie "{qkey[0:2]}{dkey[0:2]}=1"
private fun mediafireAuthCookies(streamUrl: String): String {
    return try {
        val seg = android.net.Uri.parse(streamUrl).pathSegments
        if (seg.size < 2) return ""
        val dkey = seg[0]; val qkey = seg[1]
        if (dkey.length < 2 || qkey.length < 2) return ""
        "${qkey.take(2)}${dkey.take(2)}=1; dr_$qkey=1"
    } catch (_: Exception) { "" }
}

fun detectServer(streamUrl: String, pageUrl: String?): ServerProfile {
    val combined = (streamUrl + (pageUrl ?: "")).lowercase()
    return when {
        // ✅ MEDIAFIRE primero: el token aleatorio de ~200 chars puede contener
        // por azar "voe", "hls", "mixdrop"... y colarse en el perfil equivocado.
        combined.contains("mediafire.com") -> ServerProfile.Mediafire
        // ✅ OK.RU: el MP4 viene de mycdn.me (la URL del stream NO contiene "ok.ru"),
        // por eso también se detecta por el Referer de la página
        combined.contains("ok.ru") || combined.contains("mycdn.me") -> ServerProfile.OkRu
        // ✅ DOODSTREAM: el MP4 viene del CDN cloudatacdn.com/dood.video/doodcdn.io
        // La URL del stream NO contiene "dood" — se detecta por el CDN.
        combined.contains("cloudatacdn.com") || combined.contains("dood.video") ||
                combined.contains("doodcdn.io") || combined.contains("ds2play.com") ||
                combined.contains("playmogo.com") || combined.contains("doodstream.com") ||
                combined.contains("doply.net") -> ServerProfile.Doodstream
        // ✅ MOVIEMASTER: el m3u8 viene de api-encrypted.moviemaster.lat
        combined.contains("moviemaster.lat") -> ServerProfile.MovieMaster
        combined.contains("vimeos.zip") ||
                combined.contains("vimeos.net") ||
                combined.contains("vimeus.com") -> ServerProfile.Vimeos
        combined.contains("goodstream") -> ServerProfile.GoodStream
        combined.contains("uqload") -> ServerProfile.Uqload
        combined.contains("smoothpre") -> ServerProfile.SmoothPre
        combined.contains("callistanise") -> ServerProfile.Callistanise
        combined.contains("dhtpre") -> ServerProfile.Callistanise
        // ✅ VOE: el host rota entre espejos que NO contienen "voe" en el nombre.
        // También detectamos por el CDN del stream (cloudwindow-route.com/engine/hls2).
        combined.contains("voe") || combined.contains("jessicayeahcatch") ||
                combined.contains("lindalastattack") || combined.contains("caseyseemavoid") ||
                combined.contains("tubelessceliolymph") || combined.contains("simpulumlamerop") ||
                combined.contains("urochsunloath") || combined.contains("nathanfromsubject") ||
                combined.contains("yip.su") || combined.contains("metagnathtuggers") ||
                combined.contains("donaldlineelse") || combined.contains("crystaltreatmenteast") ||
                combined.contains("voeunblock") || combined.contains("voeunbl0ck") ||
                combined.contains("cloudwindow-route.com") -> ServerProfile.Voe
        combined.contains("vidlink") -> ServerProfile.VidLink
        combined.contains("odysee") -> ServerProfile.Odysee
        combined.contains("minochinos") -> ServerProfile.MinoChinos
        combined.contains("fastream") -> ServerProfile.Fastream
        combined.contains("streamtape") -> ServerProfile.Streamtape
        combined.contains("savefiles.com") -> ServerProfile.SaveFiles
        combined.contains("streamwish") || combined.contains("playnixes") || combined.contains("niramirus") ||
                combined.contains("premilkyway") || combined.contains("medixiru") || combined.contains("ghbrisk") ||
                combined.contains("awish.pro") || combined.contains("dwish.pro") || combined.contains("embedwish") ||
                combined.contains("wishembed") || combined.contains("kswplayer") || combined.contains("wishfast") ||
                combined.contains("sfastwish") || combined.contains("strwish") || combined.contains("flaswish") ||
