import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

private fun isWifiConnected(context: Context): Boolean {
    val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
    val network = connectivityManager.activeNetwork ?: return false
    val capabilities = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return false
    return capabilities.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI)
}

private fun extractYear(title: String): String =
    Regex("\\((\\d{4})\\)").find(title)?.groupValues?.get(1) ?: ""

private fun cleanTitle(title: String): String =
    title.replace(Regex("\\(\\d{4}\\)|\\[.*?\\]|\\{.*?\\}"), "").trim()


@Composable
fun M3uMovieDetailScreen(
    movie: M3uChannel,
    listId: String,
    tmdbInfo: TmdbMediaInfo?,
    tmdbLoading: Boolean,
    allMovies: List<M3uChannel>,
    onMovieClick: (M3uChannel) -> Unit,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onMyListChanged: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val isTvDevice = remember { context.isTv() }
    val application = context.applicationContext as Application
    val sharedCatalogViewModel: SharedCatalogViewModel = viewModel(
        factory = SharedCatalogViewModelFactory(application)
    )
    val activity = context as? Activity

    fun restoreRequestedOrientation() {
        activity?.requestedOrientation = if (isTvDevice) {
            ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_UNSPECIFIED
        } else {
            ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_USER_PORTRAIT
        }
    }

    val scrollState = rememberScrollState()
    val backFocusRequester = remember(movie.url) { FocusRequester() }
    val playFocusRequester = remember(movie.url) { FocusRequester() }
    val serverSelectorFocusRequester = remember(movie.url) { FocusRequester() }
    val firstSimilarPosterFocusRequester = remember(movie.url) { FocusRequester() }
    val trackState = remember { TrackState() }
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    val liveAllMovies by sharedCatalogViewModel.moviesList.collectAsState()
    val liveMovie = remember(movie.url, liveAllMovies) {
        liveAllMovies.firstOrNull { it.url == movie.url } ?: movie
    }
    var isInMyList by remember(movie.url) {
        mutableStateOf(M3uMyListStore.contains(context, M3uMyListEntry.MOVIE, movie.url))
    }
    val movieSynopsis = tmdbInfo?.overview?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
        ?: liveMovie.sinopsis.orEmpty()
    var showFullSynopsis by remember(movie.url) { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(movie.url) {
        scrollState.scrollTo(0)
        WorkerResolver.getSessionToken(context)
    }

    var currentStreamUrl by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var currentEpTitle by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var playerKey by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    var activePlayerType by remember { mutableIntStateOf(1) }

    var isExtractingPlay by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var isExtractingCast by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showCastDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var castStreamUrl by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var castReferer by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var castCookies by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    var currentReferer by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var currentRefererFallback by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var isSilentMode by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var preExtractedCookies by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    var showServerDeadOverlay by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var serverDeadMessageText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var showNoServersOverlay by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val servers = remember(movie.url, liveMovie.servers) {
        liveMovie.servers.ifEmpty {
            listOf(M3uServer("Servidor Único", movie.url, liveMovie.isEmbed))
        }
    }

    var selectedServer by remember(movie.url, servers) { mutableStateOf(servers.first()) }
    var expandedServers by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var retryCount by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }

    var similarMoviesByApi by remember { mutableStateOf<List<M3uChannel>>(emptyList()) }

    LaunchedEffect(movie.url, allMovies.size, allMovies.hashCode()) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val snapshotMovies = allMovies
            val genre = liveMovie.genre
            val byGenre = if (!genre.isNullOrBlank()) {
                val genresList = genre.split(Regex("[,|/·]")).map { it.trim().lowercase() }
                snapshotMovies.filter {
                    it.url != movie.url &&
                            !it.genre.isNullOrBlank() &&
                            it.genre.split(Regex("[,|/·]")).any { g -> genresList.contains(g.trim().lowercase()) }
                }.shuffled()
            } else emptyList()

            val finalList = if (byGenre.size < 10) {
                val resto = snapshotMovies.filter { it.url != movie.url && !byGenre.contains(it) }.shuffled()
                (byGenre + resto).take(15)
            } else {
                byGenre.take(15)
            }

            similarMoviesByApi = finalList
        }
    }

    val historyEntry = remember(movie.url, listId) {
        M3uWatchHistoryStore.getEntry(movie.url, M3uWatchType.MOVIE)
            ?.takeIf { it.listId.isBlank() || it.listId == listId }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(movie.url, historyEntry, servers) {
        val savedServerUrl = historyEntry?.serverUrl?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
        if (savedServerUrl != null) {
            selectedServer = servers.firstOrNull { it.url == savedServerUrl } ?: selectedServer
        }
    }

    val resumePositionMs = remember(historyEntry) {
        historyEntry?.takeIf { !it.isFinished && it.positionMs > 0L }?.positionMs ?: 0L
    }

    val shouldResume = resumePositionMs > 0L

    fun saveMovieAsRecent(
        positionMs: Long = 0L,
        durationMs: Long = 0L,
        server: M3uServer? = selectedServer
    ) {
        M3uWatchHistoryStore.save(
            M3uWatchEntry(
                contentUrl = movie.url,
                watchType = M3uWatchType.MOVIE,
                title = liveMovie.title,
                logoUrl = liveMovie.logo,
                listId = listId,
                positionMs = positionMs,
                durationMs = durationMs,
                serverName = server?.name.orEmpty(),
                serverUrl = server?.url.orEmpty()
            )
        )
    }

    fun openServer(server: M3uServer, saveRecent: Boolean = true, isForCast: Boolean = false) {
        currentEpTitle = liveMovie.title

        if (saveRecent && !isForCast) {
            saveMovieAsRecent(
                positionMs = historyEntry?.positionMs ?: 0L,
                durationMs = historyEntry?.durationMs ?: 0L,
                server = server
            )
        }

        coroutineScope.launch {
            if (isForCast) isExtractingCast = true else isExtractingPlay = true

            val resolvedUrl = if (WorkerResolver.isWorkerUrl(server.url)) {
                android.util.Log.d("MOVIE_DETAIL", "🔍 Resolviendo worker en película: ${server.url}")
                WorkerResolver.resolveIfWorker(server.url, isSeries = false, context = context) ?: server.url
            } else {
                server.url
            }
            android.util.Log.d("VOD_EXTRACT", "🎬 Resolviendo al click: $resolvedUrl")
            val result = VideoExtractor.resolve(context, resolvedUrl)

            if (isForCast) {
                if (result.playerType == 3) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "Este servidor no permite Cast directo.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                } else {
                    castStreamUrl = result.targetUrl
                    castReferer = result.referer
                    castCookies = result.cookies
                    showCastDialog = true
                }
                isExtractingCast = false
            } else {
                currentStreamUrl = result.targetUrl
                currentReferer = result.referer
                currentRefererFallback = null
                isSilentMode = result.isSilentMode
                activePlayerType = result.playerType
                preExtractedCookies = result.cookies

                castStreamUrl = result.targetUrl
                castReferer = result.referer
                castCookies = result.cookies

                CookieManager.getInstance().flush()
                playerKey = server.url
                isExtractingPlay = false
            }
        }
    }

    val forceNextServer = {
        val currentIndex = servers.indexOf(selectedServer)
        if (currentIndex < servers.size - 1) {
            android.util.Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Forzando cambio al siguiente servidor (${currentIndex + 2}/${servers.size})")
            retryCount = 0
            selectedServer = servers[currentIndex + 1]
            currentStreamUrl = null
            playerKey = null
            castStreamUrl = null
            isSilentMode = false
            VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
            VodPlayerHolder.player = null
            CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                delay(150)
                openServer(selectedServer, saveRecent = false)
            }
        } else {
            android.util.Log.e("VOD_EXTRACT", "❌ No hay más servidores disponibles")
            showNoServersOverlay = true
            playerKey = null
            PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
            restoreRequestedOrientation()
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(movie.url) {
        playerKey = null
        currentStreamUrl = null
        currentReferer = null
        currentRefererFallback = null
        isSilentMode = false
        castStreamUrl = null
        retryCount = 0
        showServerDeadOverlay = false
        showNoServersOverlay = false
        serverDeadMessageText = ""

        VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
        VodPlayerHolder.player = null
        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false

        WebStorage.getInstance().deleteAllData()
        CookieManager.getInstance().removeAllCookies(null)
        CookieManager.getInstance().flush()
    }

    val triggerServerFallback = {
        if (retryCount < 3) {
            retryCount++
            Toast.makeText(context, "Reintentando... ($retryCount/3)", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            val tempUrl = currentStreamUrl
            currentStreamUrl = null
            playerKey = null

            CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                delay(800)
                currentStreamUrl = tempUrl
                playerKey = tempUrl
            }
        } else {
            // ✅ REPORTAR FALLO FINAL ANTES DE CAMBIAR DE SERVIDOR
            SupabaseFailReporter.reportCanalFail(context, movie.url, selectedServer.url)
            forceNextServer()
        }
    }

    val isFullscreen = PlayerUiState.isFullscreen

    BackHandler(enabled = true) {
        if (isFullscreen) {
            PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
            restoreRequestedOrientation()
        } else {
            onBack()
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.Black)
    ) {
        IconButton(
            onClick = {
                if (isFullscreen) {
                    PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
                    restoreRequestedOrientation()
                } else {
                    onBack()
                }
            },
            modifier = Modifier
                .focusProperties { down = playFocusRequester }
                .focusRequester(backFocusRequester)
                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                .padding(16.dp)
                .zIndex(10f)
                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.5f), CircleShape)
        ) {
            Icon(
                Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                contentDescription = "Atrás",
                tint = Color.White,
                modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
            )
        }

        if (!isFullscreen) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .verticalScroll(scrollState)
            ) {
                var bannerHasFocus by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                var playButtonHasFocus by remember(movie.url) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                var myListButtonHasFocus by remember(movie.url) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                var synopsisButtonHasFocus by remember(movie.url) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                val playButtonScale by animateFloatAsState(
                    targetValue = if (isTvDevice && playButtonHasFocus) 1.05f else 1f,
                    animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                    label = "movie_play_focus_scale"
                )
                val myListButtonScale by animateFloatAsState(
                    targetValue = if (isTvDevice && myListButtonHasFocus) 1.05f else 1f,
                    animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                    label = "movie_my_list_focus_scale"
                )
                val synopsisButtonScale by animateFloatAsState(
                    targetValue = if (isTvDevice && synopsisButtonHasFocus) 1.05f else 1f,
                    animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                    label = "movie_synopsis_focus_scale"
                )
                val myListButtonColor by animateColorAsState(
                    targetValue = if (isInMyList) Color(0xFF2E6B4F) else Color(0xFF414A56),
                    animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220),
                    label = "movie_my_list_color"
                )
                val screenConfiguration = LocalConfiguration.current
                val screenWidthDp = screenConfiguration.screenWidthDp
                val screenHeightDp = screenConfiguration.screenHeightDp
                val isLargeScreen = isTvDevice || screenWidthDp >= 600   // 📺 TV o 💻 tablet
                val openMovieFullscreen: () -> Unit = {
                    if (isLargeScreen) {
                        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = true
                        activity?.requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE
                        activity?.hideSystemBarsCompatExo()
                    }
                    openServer(selectedServer, saveRecent = true)
                }

// 📺 En TV el foco inicial cae directamente en el botón Reproducir.
                LaunchedEffect(playerKey, isTvDevice, movie.url) {
                    if (playerKey == null && isTvDevice) {
                        delay(300)
                        runCatching { playFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
                    }
                }

                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .then(
                            if (isTvDevice) {
                                Modifier.height((screenHeightDp * 0.52f).coerceIn(300f, 420f).dp)
                            } else {
                                Modifier.aspectRatio(16f / 9f)
                            }
                        )
                        // En teléfono/tablet se conserva el comportamiento anterior del banner.
                        .then(
                            if (isTvDevice) Modifier else Modifier
                                .scale(if (bannerHasFocus) 1.02f else 1f)
                                .zIndex(if (bannerHasFocus) 1f else 0f)
                                .onFocusChanged { bannerHasFocus = it.hasFocus }
                                .clickable(enabled = playerKey == null) { openMovieFullscreen() }
                        )
                        .border(
                            width = if (!isTvDevice && bannerHasFocus) 4.dp else 0.dp,
                            color = if (!isTvDevice && bannerHasFocus) Color.White else Color.Transparent,
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(if (isTvDevice) 16.dp else 12.dp)
                        )
                        .background(Color(0xFF101826))
                ) {
                    if (playerKey == null) {
                        val backdrop = tmdbInfo?.backdropPath?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: liveMovie.logo
                        val backdropRequest = remember(backdrop, movie.url, isTvDevice) {
                            ImageRequest.Builder(context)
                                .data(if (backdrop.startsWith("/")) "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/${if (isTvDevice) "original" else "w1280"}$backdrop" else backdrop)
                                .size(if (isTvDevice) 1920 else 1280)
                                .memoryCacheKey("${movie.url}_$backdrop")
                                .crossfade(true)
                                .build()
                        }
                        AsyncImage(
                            model = backdropRequest,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                            alignment = Alignment.TopCenter,
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                        )
                        Box(
                            Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                                .background(
                                    if (isTvDevice) {
                                        Brush.verticalGradient(
                                            colorStops = arrayOf(
                                                0f to Color.Transparent,
                                                0.34f to Color.Transparent,
                                                0.56f to Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                                                0.78f to Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.62f),
                                                1f to Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.96f)
                                            )
                                        )
                                    } else {
                                        Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(Color.Transparent, Color.Black.copy(0.82f)))
                                    }
                                )
                        )
                        if (isTvDevice) {
                            Box(
                                Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .background(
                                        Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                            listOf(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f), Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.22f), Color.Transparent)
                                        )
                                    )
                            )
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .align(Alignment.BottomStart)
                                    .padding(start = 32.dp, end = 32.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
                                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.Start
                            ) {
                                val heroTitle = if (!liveMovie.year.isNullOrBlank()) "${liveMovie.title} (${liveMovie.year})" else liveMovie.title
                                Text(
                                    text = heroTitle,
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    fontSize = 28.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    maxLines = 2,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                )
                                val movieTags = listOfNotNull(
                                    liveMovie.language?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() },
                                    liveMovie.quality?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                                )
                                if (movieTags.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                        movieTags.forEach { tag ->
                                            Text(
                                                text = tag,
                                                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f),
                                                fontSize = 10.sp,
                                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                    .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                                    .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                if (movieSynopsis.isNotBlank()) {
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = movieSynopsis,
                                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.94f),
                                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                                        lineHeight = 19.sp,
                                        maxLines = 2,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                                    )
                                }
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                                Row(
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Button(
                                        onClick = { if (!isExtractingPlay) openMovieFullscreen() },
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .focusProperties {
                                                up = backFocusRequester
                                                if (isTvDevice) down = serverSelectorFocusRequester
                                            }
                                            .focusRequester(playFocusRequester)
                                            .onFocusChanged { playButtonHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                            .graphicsLayer { scaleX = playButtonScale; scaleY = playButtonScale }
                                            .zIndex(if (playButtonHasFocus) 1f else 0f),
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                            containerColor = if (shouldResume) Color(0xFFE50914) else Color.White,
                                            contentColor = if (shouldResume) Color.White else Color.Black
                                        )
                                    ) {
                                        if (isExtractingPlay) {
                                            CircularProgressIndicator(
                                                color = if (shouldResume) Color.White else Color.Black,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp),
                                                strokeWidth = 2.dp
                                            )
                                        } else {
                                            Icon(Icons.Default.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                                        }
                                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                        Text(
                                            text = when {
                                                isExtractingPlay -> "Conectando…"
                                                shouldResume -> "Reanudar"
                                                else -> "Reproducir"
                                            },
                                            fontSize = 16.sp,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Button(
                                        onClick = {
                                            isInMyList = M3uMyListStore.toggle(
                                                context,
                                                M3uMyListEntry(
                                                    mediaType = M3uMyListEntry.MOVIE,
                                                    mediaId = liveMovie.url,
                                                    title = liveMovie.title,
                                                    logo = liveMovie.logo,
                                                    year = liveMovie.year.orEmpty(),
                                                    language = liveMovie.language.orEmpty(),
                                                    quality = liveMovie.quality.orEmpty()
                                                )
                                            )
                                            onMyListChanged()
                                        },
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .onFocusChanged { myListButtonHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                            .graphicsLayer { scaleX = myListButtonScale; scaleY = myListButtonScale }
                                            .zIndex(if (myListButtonHasFocus) 1f else 0f),
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = myListButtonColor, contentColor = Color.White)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(if (isInMyList) Icons.Default.Check else Icons.Default.Add, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                                        Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                        Text(if (isInMyList) "En Mi lista" else "Mi lista", fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                    }
                                    if (movieSynopsis.isNotBlank()) {
                                        TextButton(
                                            onClick = { showFullSynopsis = true },
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .onFocusChanged { synopsisButtonHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                                .graphicsLayer { scaleX = synopsisButtonScale; scaleY = synopsisButtonScale }
                                                .zIndex(if (synopsisButtonHasFocus) 1f else 0f),
                                            colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = Color.White)
                                        ) {
                                            Text("Ver sinopsis completa", fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                            ) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(72.dp)
                                        .animatedCircleGlow(isActive = bannerHasFocus && !shouldResume, color1 = Color(0xFFFF4500), color2 = Color(0xFFFFD700))
                                        .clip(CircleShape)
                                        .background(
                                            Brush.radialGradient(
                                                colors = if (shouldResume) {
                                                    listOf(Color(0xFFE50914), Color(0xFFB20710))
                                                } else {
                                                    listOf(Color(0xFFFFD700), Color(0xFFFF4500))
                                                }
                                            )
                                        ),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    if (isExtractingPlay) {
                                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp), strokeWidth = 3.dp)
                                    } else {
                                        Icon(Icons.Default.PlayArrow, contentDescription = "Reproducir", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp))
                                    }
                                }
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = if (shouldResume) "REANUDAR" else "VER PELÍCULA",
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    letterSpacing = 1.5.sp
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(52.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(Color(0xFF1E2A3A), Color(0xFF111827))))
                            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF3B82F6).copy(alpha = 0.5f), RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .focusRequester(serverSelectorFocusRequester)
                            .focusProperties {
                                if (isTvDevice) {
                                    up = playFocusRequester
                                    if (similarMoviesByApi.isNotEmpty()) down = firstSimilarPosterFocusRequester
                                }
                            }
                            .clickable { expandedServers = true }
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                            Icon(Icons.Rounded.Dns, null, tint = Color(0xFF3B82F6), modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            val lang = if (!selectedServer.language.isNullOrBlank()) " (${selectedServer.language})" else ""
                            Text("Servidor ${servers.indexOf(selectedServer) + 1}: ${selectedServer.name}$lang", color = Color.White, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                            Text("▼", color = Color.Gray, fontSize = 12.sp)
                        }
                    }

                    DropdownMenu(
                        expanded = expandedServers,
                        onDismissRequest = { expandedServers = false },
                        modifier = Modifier.background(Color(0xFF1E2A3A)).fillMaxWidth(0.9f)
                    ) {
                        servers.forEachIndexed { index, srv ->
                            val lang = if (!srv.language.isNullOrBlank()) " (${srv.language})" else ""
                            DropdownMenuItem(
                                text = { Text("Servidor ${index + 1}: ${srv.name}$lang", color = if (srv == selectedServer) Color(0xFF3B82F6) else Color.White) },
                                onClick = {
                                    if (srv != selectedServer) {
                                        selectedServer = srv
                                        expandedServers = false
                                        retryCount = 0
                                        if (playerKey != null) {
                                            currentStreamUrl = null
                                            playerKey = null
                                            castStreamUrl = null
                                            isSilentMode = false
                                            VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
                                            VodPlayerHolder.player = null
                                            CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                                                delay(150)
                                                openServer(srv, saveRecent = false)
                                            }
                                        } else {
                                            expandedServers = false
                                        }
                                    } else {
                                        expandedServers = false
                                    }
                                }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

                val displayTitle = if (!liveMovie.year.isNullOrBlank()) "${liveMovie.title} (${liveMovie.year})" else liveMovie.title
                if (!isTvDevice) {
                    Text(text = displayTitle, color = Color.White, fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp))
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                }

                if (!isTvDevice) {
                    val mobileMyListColor by animateColorAsState(
                        targetValue = if (isInMyList) Color(0xFF2E6B4F) else Color(0xFF414A56),
                        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220),
                        label = "movie_mobile_my_list_color"
                    )
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            if (!liveMovie.language.isNullOrBlank()) AssistChip(onClick = {}, label = { Text(liveMovie.language ?: "", fontSize = 10.sp) })
                            if (!liveMovie.quality.isNullOrBlank()) AssistChip(onClick = {}, label = { Text(liveMovie.quality ?: "", fontSize = 10.sp) })
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                        TextButton(
                            onClick = {
                                isInMyList = M3uMyListStore.toggle(
                                    context,
                                    M3uMyListEntry(
                                        mediaType = M3uMyListEntry.MOVIE,
                                        mediaId = liveMovie.url,
                                        title = liveMovie.title,
                                        logo = liveMovie.logo,
                                        year = liveMovie.year.orEmpty(),
                                        language = liveMovie.language.orEmpty(),
                                        quality = liveMovie.quality.orEmpty()
                                    )
                                )
                                onMyListChanged()
                            },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = mobileMyListColor),
                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(if (isInMyList) Icons.Default.Check else Icons.Default.Add, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text(if (isInMyList) "En Mi lista" else "Mi lista", fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        val isWifi = isWifiConnected(context)
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = {
                                if (castStreamUrl != null) showCastDialog = true
                                else openServer(selectedServer, saveRecent = false, isForCast = true)
                            },
                            enabled = !isExtractingCast && isWifi,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp).background(Color(0xFF1E2A3A), CircleShape)
                        ) {
                            if (isExtractingCast) CircularProgressIndicator(color = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                            else Icon(Icons.Default.CastConnected, contentDescription = "Transmitir a TV", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                val sinopsis = movieSynopsis
                if (!isTvDevice && sinopsis.isNotBlank()) {
                    Row(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top) {
                        val posterUrl = liveMovie.logo
                        if (posterUrl.isNotEmpty()) {
                            val posterRequest = remember(posterUrl) { ImageRequest.Builder(context).data(posterUrl).size(150).crossfade(false).build() }
                            AsyncImage(model = posterRequest, contentDescription = null, contentScale = ContentScale.Crop, modifier = Modifier.size(90.dp, 130.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)))
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        }
                        var synopsisTruncated by remember(movie.url, sinopsis) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = sinopsis,
                                color = Color(0xFFB0BEC5),
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                lineHeight = 22.sp,
                                maxLines = 4,
                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                onTextLayout = { synopsisTruncated = it.hasVisualOverflow }
                            )
                            if (synopsisTruncated) {
                                TextButton(
                                    onClick = { showFullSynopsis = true },
                                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 0.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Text("Ver completo", color = Color(0xFF60A5FA), fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                val similarMovies = similarMoviesByApi.distinctBy { it.url }
                if (similarMovies.isNotEmpty()) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    Text("Más títulos similares", color = Color.White, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp))
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    LazyRow(contentPadding = PaddingValues(start = 16.dp, end = 16.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                        items(similarMovies, key = { sim -> "${sim.url}_similar" }) { sim ->
                            var similarCardHasFocus by remember(sim.url) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                            val similarCardScale by animateFloatAsState(
                                targetValue = if (isTvDevice && similarCardHasFocus) 1.06f else 1f,
                                animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                                label = "similar_card_focus_scale"
                            )
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .width(140.dp)
                                    .zIndex(if (similarCardHasFocus) 1f else 0f)
                                    .graphicsLayer { scaleX = similarCardScale; scaleY = similarCardScale }
                                    .onFocusChanged { similarCardHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                    .then(if (sim.url == similarMovies.first().url) Modifier.focusRequester(firstSimilarPosterFocusRequester) else Modifier)
                                    .clickable { onMovieClick(sim) },
                                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                            ) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .aspectRatio(2f / 3f)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                ) {
                                    val simPosterRequest = remember(sim.logo) {
                                        ImageRequest.Builder(context).data(sim.logo.ifBlank { null }).size(150).crossfade(false).build()
                                    }
                                    AsyncImage(model = simPosterRequest, contentDescription = null, contentScale = ContentScale.Crop, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
                                }
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = cleanTitle(sim.title),
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    fontWeight = if (similarCardHasFocus) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                    maxLines = 2,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                    lineHeight = 16.sp,
                                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(32.dp)
                                )
                                val year = sim.year ?: extractYear(sim.title)
                                Text(
                                    text = year,
                                    color = Color(0xFF9FB0CC),
                                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                                    lineHeight = 14.sp,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(14.dp)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(32.dp))
            }
        }

        if (showFullSynopsis && movieSynopsis.isNotBlank()) {
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { showFullSynopsis = false },
                title = { Text("Sinopsis completa", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
                text = {
                    Text(
                        text = movieSynopsis,
                        color = Color(0xFFCFD8DC),
                        modifier = Modifier.heightIn(max = 360.dp).verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                    )
                },
                confirmButton = { TextButton(onClick = { showFullSynopsis = false }) { Text("Cerrar") } },
                containerColor = Color(0xFF1E2A3A),
                titleContentColor = Color.White,
                textContentColor = Color(0xFFCFD8DC)
            )
        }

        if (showCastDialog && castStreamUrl != null) {
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { showCastDialog = false },
                title = { Text("Transmisión Lista", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
                text = { Text("El video ha sido extraído correctamente. ¿Deseas abrir Web Video Caster para enviarlo a tu TV?") },
                confirmButton = {
                    Button(onClick = {
                        showCastDialog = false
                        ExternalCaster.castViaWebcaster(context = context, videoUrl = castStreamUrl!!, title = currentEpTitle.ifEmpty { liveMovie.title }, referer = castReferer, cookies = castCookies)
                    }, colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFF3B82F6))) { Text("Aceptar", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }
                },
                dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { showCastDialog = false }) { Text("Cancelar", color = Color.LightGray) } },
                containerColor = Color(0xFF1E2A3A),
                titleContentColor = Color.White,
                textContentColor = Color(0xFFCFD8DC)
            )
        }

        if (playerKey != null && currentStreamUrl != null) {
            val playerModifier = if (isFullscreen) Modifier.fillMaxSize().zIndex(100f) else Modifier.fillMaxWidth().aspectRatio(16f / 9f).align(Alignment.TopCenter)

            key(playerKey) {
                Box(modifier = playerModifier.background(Color.Black)) {
                    when (activePlayerType) {
                        1 -> {
                            ExoVideoPlayer(
                                url = currentStreamUrl!!,
                                referer = currentReferer,
                                channelName = currentEpTitle,
                                resumePositionMs = resumePositionMs,
                                preExtractedCookies = preExtractedCookies,
                                sharedViewModel = sharedCatalogViewModel,
                                refererFallback = currentRefererFallback ?: "",
                                originalEmbedUrl = selectedServer.url,
                                onHardHttpError = { code ->
                                    android.util.Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Error HTTP duro $code en ExoPlayer. Reportando...")
                                    SupabaseFailReporter.reportCanalFail(context, movie.url, selectedServer.url)
                                },
                                onProgressUpdate = { positionMs, durationMs ->
                                    M3uWatchHistoryStore.updatePosition(contentUrl = movie.url, watchType = M3uWatchType.MOVIE, positionMs = positionMs, durationMs = durationMs)
                                },
                                onBackClick = {
                                    PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
                                    restoreRequestedOrientation()
                                    playerKey = null
                                    WebStorage.getInstance().deleteAllData()
                                    CookieManager.getInstance().removeAllCookies(null)
                                    CookieManager.getInstance().flush()
                                }
                            )
                        }
                        2 -> {
                            AppVideoPlayer(
                                streamUrl = currentStreamUrl,
                                isLive = false,
                                channelName = currentEpTitle,
                                resumePositionMs = resumePositionMs,
                                trackState = trackState,
                                referer = currentReferer,
                                onError = { triggerServerFallback() },
                                onProgressUpdate = { positionMs, durationMs ->
                                    M3uWatchHistoryStore.updatePosition(contentUrl = movie.url, watchType = M3uWatchType.MOVIE, positionMs = positionMs, durationMs = durationMs)
                                },
                                onBackClick = {
                                    val pos = VodPlayerHolder.player?.currentPosition ?: 0L
                                    val dur = VodPlayerHolder.player?.duration ?: 0L
                                    M3uWatchHistoryStore.save(M3uWatchEntry(contentUrl = movie.url, watchType = M3uWatchType.MOVIE, title = liveMovie.title, logoUrl = liveMovie.logo, listId = listId, positionMs = pos, durationMs = dur, serverName = selectedServer.name, serverUrl = selectedServer.url))
                                    if (isFullscreen) {
                                        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
                                        restoreRequestedOrientation()
                                    } else {
                                        playerKey = null
                                        WebStorage.getInstance().deleteAllData()
                                        CookieManager.getInstance().removeAllCookies(null)
                                        CookieManager.getInstance().flush()
                                    }
                                }
                            )
                        }
                        3 -> {
                            EmbedVideoPlayer(
                                url = currentStreamUrl!!,
                                canalUrl = movie.url,
                                isSilentMode = isSilentMode,
                                fanartUrl = "",
                                refererOverride = currentReferer,     // ═══ NUEVO: pasar Referer al iframe ═══
                                onTimeoutError = {
                                    // ✅ MOSTRAR OVERLAY Y FORZAR CAMBIO DE SERVIDOR
                                    android.util.Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Timeout o Video no encontrado en: ${selectedServer.name}")
                                    serverDeadMessageText = "El servidor \"${selectedServer.name}\" no está disponible.\nCambiando de fuente…"
                                    showServerDeadOverlay = true
                                    CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                                        delay(1500)
                                        showServerDeadOverlay = false
                                        forceNextServer()
                                    }
                                },
                                onEmbedUnavailable = {
                                    android.util.Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Servidor caído detectado: ${selectedServer.name}")
                                    SupabaseFailReporter.reportCanalFail(context, movie.url, selectedServer.url)
                                    serverDeadMessageText = "El servidor \"${selectedServer.name}\" no está disponible.\nCambiando de fuente…"
                                    showServerDeadOverlay = true
                                    CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                                        delay(1500)
                                        showServerDeadOverlay = false
                                        forceNextServer()
                                    }
                                },
                                onM3u8Intercepted = { interceptedUrl, cookies ->
                                    android.util.Log.d("VOD_EXTRACT", "🔍 Validando stream: $interceptedUrl")

                                    val isDoodstreamStream =
                                        interceptedUrl.contains("token=", ignoreCase = true) && (
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("cloudatacdn.com", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("dood.video", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("doodcdn.io", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("ds2play.com", ignoreCase = true)
                                                )

                                    val isIbraStream =
                                        interceptedUrl.contains("ibra.lat", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("cloudwindow-route.com", ignoreCase = true)

                                    val isIbraMaster = isIbraStream && interceptedUrl.contains("?s=", ignoreCase = true)
                                    val isIbraVariantM3u8 = isIbraStream && interceptedUrl.substringBefore("?").endsWith(".m3u8", ignoreCase = true)

                                    val isValidStream = isIbraStream || isDoodstreamStream ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("master.m3u8", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("master.txt", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains(".urlset/", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("hls2-c", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.substringBefore("?").endsWith(".m3u8", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.substringBefore("?").endsWith(".mp4", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains(".mp4?", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("mxcontent.net", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("dropcdn.io", ignoreCase = true)

                                    android.util.Log.d("VOD_EXTRACT", "🔍 isValidStream=$isValidStream (dood=$isDoodstreamStream, ibra=$isIbraStream, master=$isIbraMaster, variant=$isIbraVariantM3u8)")

                                    if (isValidStream) {
                                        val alreadyOnIbraMaster = currentStreamUrl?.let { it.contains("ibra.lat") && it.contains("?s=", ignoreCase = true) } == true
                                        val shouldOverrideWithVariant = isIbraVariantM3u8 && alreadyOnIbraMaster

                                        if (shouldOverrideWithVariant) {
                                            android.util.Log.d("VOD_EXTRACT", "🔁 Reemplazando master ?s= por variante .m3u8 (más rápida/robusta)")
                                        } else if (alreadyOnIbraMaster && isIbraMaster) {
                                            android.util.Log.d("VOD_EXTRACT", "⏭️ Ignorando master duplicado, ya estamos en ExoPlayer")
                                            return@EmbedVideoPlayer
                                        }

                                        preExtractedCookies = cookies
                                        isSilentMode = false
                                        currentStreamUrl = interceptedUrl

                                        if (interceptedUrl.contains("mxcontent.net", ignoreCase = true)) {
                                            val embedOrigin = try {
                                                val u = java.net.URI(selectedServer?.url ?: "")
                                                if (u.scheme != null && u.host != null) "${u.scheme}://${u.host}/" else "https://mxdrop.click/"
                                            } catch (_: Exception) { "https://mxdrop.click/" }
                                            currentReferer = embedOrigin
                                            currentRefererFallback = "https://miiixdrop.net/"
                                            android.util.Log.d("VOD_EXTRACT", "🎯 mxcontent.net → Referer=$embedOrigin, fallback=miiixdrop.net")
                                        } else {
                                            currentReferer = when {
                                                isIbraStream -> when {
                                                    selectedServer?.url?.contains("nupload", ignoreCase = true) == true -> "https://nupload.my/"
                                                    currentReferer?.contains("nupload", ignoreCase = true) == true -> "https://nupload.my/"
                                                    else -> currentReferer
                                                }
                                                isDoodstreamStream -> when {
                                                    currentReferer?.contains("playmogo.com", ignoreCase = true) == true -> "https://playmogo.com/"
                                                    currentReferer?.contains("doodstream.com", ignoreCase = true) == true -> "https://doodstream.com/"
                                                    currentReferer?.contains("dood.video", ignoreCase = true) == true -> "https://dood.video/"
                                                    currentReferer?.contains("ds2play.com", ignoreCase = true) == true -> "https://ds2play.com/"
                                                    else -> currentReferer
                                                }
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("dropcdn.io", ignoreCase = true) -> "https://dr0pstream.com/"
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("streamwish", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("playnixes", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("hgplaycdn", ignoreCase = true) -> "https://streamwish.to/"
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("callistanise", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("affordablewebinars", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("vidhide", ignoreCase = true) -> "https://callistanise.com/"
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("hanerix", ignoreCase = true) -> "https://hanerix.com/"
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("sagaciousslumber.site", ignoreCase = true) -> "https://cloudorchestranova.com/"
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("cloudorchestranova.com", ignoreCase = true) -> "https://vsembed.ru/"
                                                else -> currentReferer
                                            }
                                            currentRefererFallback = null
                                        }

                                        android.util.Log.d("VOD_EXTRACT", "✅ Transicionando a ExoPlayer: playerType=1, referer=$currentReferer, url=$interceptedUrl")
                                        activePlayerType = 1
                                    } else {
                                        android.util.Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Stream rechazado por validador: $interceptedUrl")
                                    }
                                },
                                onBackClick = {
                                    M3uWatchHistoryStore.save(M3uWatchEntry(contentUrl = movie.url, watchType = M3uWatchType.MOVIE, title = liveMovie.title, logoUrl = liveMovie.logo, listId = listId, positionMs = historyEntry?.positionMs ?: 0L, durationMs = historyEntry?.durationMs ?: 0L, serverName = selectedServer.name, serverUrl = selectedServer.url))
                                    if (isFullscreen) {
                                        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
                                        restoreRequestedOrientation()
                                    } else {
                                        playerKey = null
                                        WebStorage.getInstance().deleteAllData()
                                        CookieManager.getInstance().removeAllCookies(null)
                                        CookieManager.getInstance().flush()
                                    }
                                }
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (showServerDeadOverlay) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.92f)).zIndex(50f),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Column(
                                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(32.dp)
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Default.Warning, contentDescription = null, tint = Color(0xFFFFC107), modifier = Modifier.size(72.dp))
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                                Text(text = serverDeadMessageText, color = Color.White, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(24.dp))
                                CircularProgressIndicator(color = Color(0xFFE50914))
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (showNoServersOverlay) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.96f)).zIndex(200f),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Column(
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(32.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Warning, contentDescription = null, tint = Color(0xFFE50914), modifier = Modifier.size(80.dp))
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "Servidores no disponibles",
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontSize = 22.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                    )
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "Por el momento nuestros servidores no se encuentran disponibles.\nIntenta de nuevo más tarde.",
                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.75f),
                        fontSize = 15.sp,
                        lineHeight = 22.sp,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                    )
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(28.dp))
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            showNoServersOverlay = false
                            playerKey = null
                            currentStreamUrl = null
                            onBack()
                        },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFE50914))
                    ) {
                        Text("Volver", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.latinoiptvpro.ui.m3u

@Suppress("DEPRECATION")
private fun Activity.showSystemBarsCompat() {
    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
        window.insetsController?.show(WindowInsets.Type.systemBars())
    } else {
        window.decorView.systemUiVisibility = View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_VISIBLE
    }
}

private fun isWifiConnected(context: Context): Boolean {
    val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
    val network = connectivityManager.activeNetwork ?: return false
    val capabilities = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return false
    return capabilities.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI)
}

@Composable
fun Modifier.animatedGlowBorder(
    isActive: Boolean,
    color1: Color = Color(0xFFFF4500),
    color2: Color = Color(0xFFFFD700),
    animationDuration: Int = 1500
): Modifier {
    if (!isActive) return this
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "glow_border")
    val alpha by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.4f,
        targetValue = 1f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(animationDuration, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "glow_alpha"
    )
    val scale by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.98f,
        targetValue = 1.02f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(animationDuration, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "glow_scale"
    )
    return this
        .scale(scale)
        .border(
            width = 2.dp,
            brush = Brush.horizontalGradient(
                colors = listOf(color1.copy(alpha = alpha), color2.copy(alpha = alpha), color1.copy(alpha = alpha))
            ),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
        )
        .shadow(
            elevation = 8.dp,
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
            ambientColor = color1.copy(alpha = alpha),
            spotColor = color2.copy(alpha = alpha)
        )
}

@Composable
fun Modifier.animatedCircleGlow(
    isActive: Boolean,
    color1: Color = Color(0xFFFF4500),
    color2: Color = Color(0xFFFFD700),
    animationDuration: Int = 1500
): Modifier {
    if (!isActive) return this
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "circle_glow")
    val alpha by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.4f,
        targetValue = 1f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(animationDuration, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "circle_alpha"
    )
    val scale by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.95f,
        targetValue = 1.05f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(animationDuration, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "circle_scale"
    )
    return this
        .scale(scale)
        .border(
            width = 3.dp,
            brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                colors = listOf(color2.copy(alpha = alpha), color1.copy(alpha = alpha), color2.copy(alpha = alpha))
            ),
            shape = CircleShape
        )
        .shadow(
            elevation = 12.dp,
            shape = CircleShape,
            ambientColor = color1.copy(alpha = alpha),
            spotColor = color2.copy(alpha = alpha)
        )
}

@Composable
private fun EpisodeItem(
    episode: M3uChannel,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    listId: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    focusRequester: FocusRequester? = null,
    onMoveUp: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    onMoveDown: (() -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val isTvDevice = remember { context.isTv() }
    var hasFocus by remember(episode.url) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val focusScale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isTvDevice && hasFocus) 1.02f else 1f,
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
        label = "episode_focus_scale"
    )
    val entry = remember(episode.url, listId) {
        M3uWatchHistoryStore.getEntry(episode.url, M3uWatchType.SERIES)
            ?.takeIf { it.listId.isBlank() || it.listId == listId }
    }
    val progress = entry?.takeIf { !it.isFinished }?.progressPercent ?: 0
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
            .zIndex(if (hasFocus) 1f else 0f)
            .graphicsLayer { scaleX = focusScale; scaleY = focusScale }
            .then(if (focusRequester != null) Modifier.focusRequester(focusRequester) else Modifier)
            .onKeyEvent { event ->
                if (!isTvDevice || event.type != KeyEventType.KeyDown) {
                    false
                } else if (event.nativeKeyEvent.repeatCount > 0) {
                    true
                } else when (event.key) {
                    Key.DirectionUp -> { onMoveUp?.invoke(); true }
                    Key.DirectionDown -> { onMoveDown?.invoke(); true }
                    else -> false
                }
            }
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .animatedGlowBorder(isActive = isSelected && !isTvDevice, color1 = Color(0xFFFF4500), color2 = Color(0xFFFFD700))
            .background(
                when {
                    isTvDevice && hasFocus -> Color(0xFF2E415C)
                    isSelected -> Color(0xFF1B2636)
                    else -> Color(0xFF111111)
                }
            )
            .then(
                if (isTvDevice) Modifier.border(
                    width = if (hasFocus) 2.dp else if (isSelected) 1.dp else 0.dp,
                    color = if (hasFocus) Color.White else Color(0xFF36D760),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                ) else Modifier
            )
            .onFocusChanged { hasFocus = it.isFocused }
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                indication = null,
                onClick = onClick
            )
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp)
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .width(82.dp)
                    .height(54.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                    .background(Color(0xFF202020)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                if (episode.logo.isNotBlank()) {
                    val episodeImageRequest = remember(episode.logo, episode.url) {
                        ImageRequest.Builder(context)
                            .data(episode.logo)
                            .size(100)
                            .memoryCacheKey("${episode.url}_${episode.logo}")
                            .crossfade(false)
                            .build()
                    }
                    AsyncImage(
                        model = episodeImageRequest,
                        contentDescription = episode.title,
                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                    )
                } else {
                    Text(
                        text = "EP",
                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                }
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    text = episode.title,
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = if (isSelected || hasFocus) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                    maxLines = 2,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    if (!episode.language.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        Text(
                            text = episode.language ?: "",
                            color = Color(0xFFB0BEC5),
                            fontSize = 11.sp
                        )
                    }
                    if (!episode.quality.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        Text(
                            text = episode.quality ?: "",
                            color = Color(0xFF90CAF9),
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                        )
                    }
                    if (progress > 0) {
                        Text(
                            text = "$progress%",
                            color = Color(0xFF81C784),
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (progress > 0) {
            LinearProgressIndicator(
                progress = { progress / 100f },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(3.dp),
                color = Color(0xFF4CAF50),
                trackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f)
            )
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun M3uSeriesDetailScreen(
    seriesName: String,
    listId: String,
    episodesFlatList: List<M3uChannel>,
    tmdbInfo: TmdbMediaInfo?,
    tmdbLoading: Boolean,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onMyListChanged: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val isTvDevice = remember { context.isTv() }
    var isInMyList by remember(seriesName) {
        mutableStateOf(M3uMyListStore.contains(context, M3uMyListEntry.SERIES, seriesName))
    }
    val application = context.applicationContext as Application
    val sharedCatalogViewModel: SharedCatalogViewModel = viewModel(
        factory = SharedCatalogViewModelFactory(application)
    )
    val activity = context as? Activity

    fun restoreRequestedOrientation() {
        activity?.requestedOrientation = if (isTvDevice) {
            ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_UNSPECIFIED
        } else {
            ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_USER_PORTRAIT
        }
    }

    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val listState = rememberLazyListState()
    val backFocusRequester = remember(seriesName) { FocusRequester() }
    val playFocusRequester = remember(seriesName) { FocusRequester() }
    val serverSelectorFocusRequester = remember(seriesName) { FocusRequester() }
    val seasonSelectorFocusRequester = remember(seriesName) { FocusRequester() }
    val firstEpisodeFocusRequester = remember(seriesName) { FocusRequester() }
    val episodeFocusRequesters = remember(seriesName) { mutableMapOf<String, FocusRequester>() }
    fun episodeFocusKey(episode: M3uChannel): String =
        "${episode.url}_${episode.seasonNum}_${episode.episodeNum}"
    val trackState = remember { TrackState() }

    val liveAllSeries by sharedCatalogViewModel.seriesMap.collectAsState()
    val liveEpisodes = remember(seriesName, liveAllSeries) {
        liveAllSeries[seriesName] ?: episodesFlatList
    }

    LaunchedEffect(seriesName) {
        WorkerResolver.getSessionToken(context)
    }

    val sortedEpisodes by remember(liveEpisodes) {
        derivedStateOf {
            liveEpisodes
                .distinctBy { "${it.seasonNum}_${it.episodeNum}_${it.cleanTitle.lowercase()}" }
                .sortedWith(
                    compareBy<M3uChannel> { it.seasonNum }
                        .thenBy { it.episodeNum }
                        .thenBy { it.title }
                )
        }
    }

    val groupedBySeason by remember(sortedEpisodes) {
        derivedStateOf {
            sortedEpisodes.groupBy { episode ->
                episode.group.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "Temporada ${episode.seasonNum.coerceAtLeast(1)}"
            }
        }
    }

    val seasonNames by remember(groupedBySeason) {
        derivedStateOf { groupedBySeason.keys.toList() }
    }

    val episodesCountBySeason by remember(groupedBySeason) {
        derivedStateOf { groupedBySeason.mapValues { it.value.size } }
    }

    val lastWatchedEntry = remember(seriesName, listId) {
        M3uWatchHistoryStore.getSeries()
            .firstOrNull {
                (it.listId.isBlank() || it.listId == listId) &&
                        it.seriesName.equals(seriesName, ignoreCase = true)
            }
    }

    var selectedEpisode by remember { mutableStateOf<M3uChannel?>(null) }
    var selectedSeasonName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var initialSelectionDone by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var initialTvPlayFocusRequested by remember(seriesName) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var episodeFocusMoveInProgress by remember(seriesName) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    fun focusEpisodeAt(index: Int) {
        if (episodeFocusMoveInProgress) return
        val episodes = groupedBySeason[selectedSeasonName].orEmpty()
        if (index !in episodes.indices) return
        val episode = episodes[index]
        val requester = if (index == 0) {
            firstEpisodeFocusRequester
        } else {
            episodeFocusRequesters.getOrPut(episodeFocusKey(episode)) { FocusRequester() }
        }
        episodeFocusMoveInProgress = true
        scope.launch {
            try {
                val focused = runCatching { requester.requestFocus() }.getOrDefault(false)
                if (!focused) {
                    runCatching { listState.scrollToItem(index + 1) }
                    delay(80)
                    runCatching { requester.requestFocus() }
                }
                delay(100)
            } finally {
                episodeFocusMoveInProgress = false
            }
        }
    }
    var showFullSynopsis by remember(seriesName) { mutableStateOf(false) }
