    val seriesSynopsis = tmdbInfo?.overview?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
        ?: selectedEpisode?.sinopsis.orEmpty()

    LaunchedEffect(sortedEpisodes.isNotEmpty(), lastWatchedEntry) {
        if (sortedEpisodes.isNotEmpty() && !initialSelectionDone) {
            val lastEpisode = lastWatchedEntry?.let { entry ->
                sortedEpisodes.firstOrNull { it.url == entry.contentUrl }
            }

            selectedEpisode = lastEpisode ?: sortedEpisodes.firstOrNull()

            val lastSeasonName = lastEpisode?.group?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                ?: "Temporada ${(lastEpisode?.seasonNum ?: 1).coerceAtLeast(1)}"

            selectedSeasonName = if (lastSeasonName in seasonNames) {
                lastSeasonName
            } else {
                seasonNames.firstOrNull() ?: ""
            }

            initialSelectionDone = true
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(selectedSeasonName, groupedBySeason) {
        if (selectedSeasonName.isNotBlank() && groupedBySeason.isNotEmpty()) {
            val episodesOfSeason = groupedBySeason[selectedSeasonName] ?: emptyList()
            if (episodesOfSeason.isNotEmpty() && selectedEpisode !in episodesOfSeason) {
                selectedEpisode = episodesOfSeason.firstOrNull()
            }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(sortedEpisodes) {
        val currentUrl = selectedEpisode?.url ?: return@LaunchedEffect
        val fresh = sortedEpisodes.firstOrNull { it.url == currentUrl }
        if (fresh != null && fresh !== selectedEpisode) {
            selectedEpisode = fresh
            Log.d("SERIES_DETAIL", "♻️ Episodio seleccionado actualizado: ${fresh.title}")
        }
    }

    var currentStreamUrl by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var currentEpTitle by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var playerKey by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    var activePlayerType by remember { mutableIntStateOf(1) }

    var currentReferer by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var currentRefererFallback by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var isSilentMode by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    var isExtractingPlay by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var isExtractingCast by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    var showCastDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var castStreamUrl by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var castReferer by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var castCookies by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    var retryCount by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var preExtractedCookies by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    var showServerDeadOverlay by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var serverDeadMessageText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var showNoServersOverlay by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val servers = remember(selectedEpisode?.url, selectedEpisode?.servers) {
        val ep = selectedEpisode ?: return@remember emptyList()
        ep.servers.ifEmpty {
            listOf(M3uServer("Servidor Único", ep.url, ep.isEmbed, ep.language))
        }
    }

    var selectedServer by remember(selectedEpisode?.url, servers) {
        mutableStateOf(servers.firstOrNull())
    }
    var expandedServers by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(initialSelectionDone, lastWatchedEntry, selectedEpisode) {
        if (initialSelectionDone && selectedEpisode != null && lastWatchedEntry != null) {
            val savedServerUrl = lastWatchedEntry?.serverUrl
            if (!savedServerUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                val savedServer = selectedEpisode?.servers?.firstOrNull { it.url == savedServerUrl }
                if (savedServer != null) {
                    selectedServer = savedServer
                }
            }
        }
    }

    val selectedEntry = remember(selectedEpisode?.url, listId) {
        selectedEpisode?.url?.let { url ->
            M3uWatchHistoryStore.getEntry(url, M3uWatchType.SERIES)
                ?.takeIf { it.listId.isBlank() || it.listId == listId }
        }
    }

    val resumePositionMs = remember(selectedEntry) {
        selectedEntry
            ?.takeIf { !it.isFinished && it.positionMs > 0L }
            ?.positionMs ?: 0L
    }

    fun saveEpisodeHistory(
        episode: M3uChannel,
        server: M3uServer?,
        positionMs: Long = 0L,
        durationMs: Long = 0L
    ) {
        M3uWatchHistoryStore.save(
            M3uWatchEntry(
                contentUrl = episode.url,
                watchType = M3uWatchType.SERIES,
                title = episode.title,
                logoUrl = episode.logo,
                listId = listId,
                seriesName = seriesName,
                seasonGroup = episode.group.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                    ?: "Temporada ${episode.seasonNum.coerceAtLeast(1)}",
                positionMs = positionMs,
                durationMs = durationMs,
                serverName = server?.name ?: "",
                serverUrl = server?.url ?: ""
            )
        )
    }

    fun openEpisodeServer(
        episode: M3uChannel,
        server: M3uServer,
        saveRecent: Boolean = true,
        isForCast: Boolean = false
    ) {
        currentEpTitle = episode.title

        if (saveRecent && !isForCast) {
            val last = M3uWatchHistoryStore.getEntry(episode.url, M3uWatchType.SERIES)
            saveEpisodeHistory(
                episode = episode,
                server = server,
                positionMs = last?.positionMs ?: 0L,
                durationMs = last?.durationMs ?: 0L
            )
        }

        scope.launch {
            if (isForCast) isExtractingCast = true else isExtractingPlay = true

            val resolvedUrl = if (WorkerResolver.isWorkerUrl(server.url)) {
                Log.d("SERIES_DETAIL", "🔍 Resolviendo worker en episodio: ${server.url}")
                WorkerResolver.resolveIfWorker(server.url, isSeries = true, context = context) ?: server.url
            } else {
                server.url
            }

            val result = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                VideoExtractor.resolve(context, resolvedUrl)
            }

            val finalReferer = if (result.targetUrl?.contains("dailymotion", ignoreCase = true) == true ||
                server.url.contains("dailymotion", ignoreCase = true)) {
                Log.d("SERIES_DAILYMOTION", "Forzando referer Dailymotion: https://geo.dailymotion.com/")
                "https://geo.dailymotion.com/"
            } else {
                result.referer
            }

            if (isForCast) {
                if (result.playerType == 3) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "Este servidor no permite Cast directo.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                } else {
                    castStreamUrl = result.targetUrl
                    castReferer = finalReferer
                    castCookies = result.cookies
                    showCastDialog = true
                }
                isExtractingCast = false
            } else {
                currentStreamUrl = result.targetUrl
                currentReferer = finalReferer
                currentRefererFallback = null
                isSilentMode = result.isSilentMode
                activePlayerType = result.playerType
                preExtractedCookies = result.cookies

                CookieManager.getInstance().flush()
                playerKey = server.url
                isExtractingPlay = false
            }
        }
    }

    val forceNextServer = {
        val currentIndex = selectedServer?.let { servers.indexOf(it) } ?: -1
        if (currentIndex in 0 until servers.lastIndex) {
            Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Forzando cambio al siguiente servidor (${currentIndex + 2}/${servers.size})")
            retryCount = 0
            selectedServer = servers[currentIndex + 1]
            currentStreamUrl = null
            playerKey = null
            isSilentMode = false
            VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
            VodPlayerHolder.player = null

            CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                delay(150)
                selectedEpisode?.let { ep ->
                    selectedServer?.let { srv ->
                        openEpisodeServer(ep, srv, saveRecent = false)
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            Log.e("VOD_EXTRACT", "❌ No hay más servidores disponibles")
            showNoServersOverlay = true
            playerKey = null
            PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
            restoreRequestedOrientation()
        }
    }

    val triggerServerFallback = {
        if (retryCount < 3) {
            retryCount++
            Toast.makeText(context, "Reintentando... ($retryCount/3)", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            val tempUrl = currentStreamUrl
            currentStreamUrl = null
            playerKey = null

            scope.launch {
                delay(800)
                currentStreamUrl = tempUrl
                playerKey = tempUrl
            }
        } else {
            // ✅ REPORTAR FALLO FINAL ANTES DE CAMBIAR DE SERVIDOR
            val epUrl = selectedEpisode?.url ?: ""
            val srvUrl = selectedServer?.url ?: ""
            if (epUrl.isNotBlank() && srvUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                SupabaseFailReporter.reportCanalFail(context, epUrl, srvUrl)
            }
            forceNextServer()
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(seriesName) {
        currentStreamUrl = null
        currentEpTitle = ""
        playerKey = null
        currentReferer = null
        currentRefererFallback = null
        isSilentMode = false
        isExtractingPlay = false
        isExtractingCast = false
        showCastDialog = false
        castStreamUrl = null
        castReferer = null
        castCookies = ""
        retryCount = 0
        showServerDeadOverlay = false
        showNoServersOverlay = false
        serverDeadMessageText = ""

        VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
        VodPlayerHolder.player = null
        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false

        WebStorage.getInstance().deleteAllData()
        CookieManager.getInstance().removeAllCookies(null)
        CookieManager.getInstance().flush()
    }

    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose {
            VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
            VodPlayerHolder.player = null
            PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
        }
    }

    fun requestEpisodeListFocus() {
        val episode = selectedEpisode
        val episodes = groupedBySeason[selectedSeasonName].orEmpty()
        val index = episodes.indexOfFirst { episode != null && episodeFocusKey(it) == episodeFocusKey(episode) }
        if (episode == null || index < 0) {
            scope.launch {
                delay(250)
                runCatching { playFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
            }
            return
        }
        focusEpisodeAt(index)
    }

    fun leaveFullscreenAndKeepPlayback() {
        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
        restoreRequestedOrientation()
        activity?.showSystemBarsCompatExo()
        scope.launch {
            delay(250)
            requestEpisodeListFocus()
        }
    }

    fun stopPlaybackAndReturnToEpisodes() {
        selectedEpisode?.let { episode ->
            val savedProgress = M3uWatchHistoryStore.getEntry(episode.url, M3uWatchType.SERIES)
            saveEpisodeHistory(
                episode = episode,
                server = selectedServer,
                positionMs = VodPlayerHolder.player?.currentPosition ?: savedProgress?.positionMs ?: 0L,
                durationMs = VodPlayerHolder.player?.duration ?: savedProgress?.durationMs ?: 0L
            )
        }
        PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = false
        restoreRequestedOrientation()
        activity?.showSystemBarsCompatExo()
        currentStreamUrl = null
        playerKey = null
        isExtractingPlay = false
        VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
        VodPlayerHolder.player = null
        scope.launch {
            delay(250)
            requestEpisodeListFocus()
        }
    }

    val isFullscreen = PlayerUiState.isFullscreen

    BackHandler(enabled = true) {
        if (isFullscreen && !isTvDevice) {
            leaveFullscreenAndKeepPlayback()
        } else if (playerKey != null) {
            stopPlaybackAndReturnToEpisodes()
        } else {
            onBack()
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.Black)
    ) {
        if (!isFullscreen) {
            IconButton(
                onClick = { onBack() },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .focusProperties { down = playFocusRequester }
                    .focusRequester(backFocusRequester)
                    .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                    .padding(16.dp)
                    .zIndex(10f)
                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.5f), CircleShape)
            ) {
                Icon(
                    Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                    contentDescription = "Atrás",
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                )
            }
        }

        if (!isFullscreen) {
            LazyColumn(
                state = listState,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
            ) {
                item {
                    var bannerHasFocus by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    var playButtonHasFocus by remember(seriesName) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    var myListButtonHasFocus by remember(seriesName) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    var synopsisButtonHasFocus by remember(seriesName) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    val playButtonScale by animateFloatAsState(
                        targetValue = if (isTvDevice && playButtonHasFocus) 1.05f else 1f,
                        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                        label = "series_play_focus_scale"
                    )
                    val myListButtonScale by animateFloatAsState(
                        targetValue = if (isTvDevice && myListButtonHasFocus) 1.05f else 1f,
                        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                        label = "series_my_list_focus_scale"
                    )
                    val synopsisButtonScale by animateFloatAsState(
                        targetValue = if (isTvDevice && synopsisButtonHasFocus) 1.05f else 1f,
                        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                        label = "series_synopsis_focus_scale"
                    )
                    val myListButtonColor by animateColorAsState(
                        targetValue = if (isInMyList) Color(0xFF2E6B4F) else Color(0xFF414A56),
                        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220),
                        label = "series_my_list_color"
                    )
                    val screenConfiguration = LocalConfiguration.current
                    val screenWidthDp = screenConfiguration.screenWidthDp
                    val screenHeightDp = screenConfiguration.screenHeightDp
                    val isLargeScreen = isTvDevice || screenWidthDp >= 600   // 📺 TV o 💻 tablet
                    val openEpisodeFullscreen: () -> Unit = {
                        if (selectedServer != null && selectedEpisode != null) {
                            if (isLargeScreen) {
                                PlayerUiState.isFullscreen = true
                                activity?.requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE
                                activity?.hideSystemBarsCompatExo()
                            }
                            openEpisodeServer(
                                episode = selectedEpisode!!,
                                server = selectedServer!!,
                                saveRecent = true
                            )
                        }
                    }

// 📺 En TV el foco inicial cae directamente en el botón Reproducir.
                    LaunchedEffect(isTvDevice, seriesName) {
                        if (isTvDevice && !initialTvPlayFocusRequested) {
                            delay(300)
                            runCatching { playFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
                            initialTvPlayFocusRequested = true
                        }
                    }

                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .then(
                                if (isTvDevice) {
                                    Modifier.height((screenHeightDp * 0.52f).coerceIn(300f, 420f).dp)
                                } else {
                                    Modifier.aspectRatio(16f / 9f)
                                }
                            )
                            // En teléfono/tablet se conserva el comportamiento anterior del banner.
                            .then(
                                if (isTvDevice) Modifier else Modifier
                                    .scale(if (bannerHasFocus) 1.02f else 1f)
                                    .zIndex(if (bannerHasFocus) 1f else 0f)
                                    .onFocusChanged { bannerHasFocus = it.hasFocus }
                                    .clickable(enabled = playerKey == null) { openEpisodeFullscreen() }
                            )
                            .border(
                                width = if (!isTvDevice && bannerHasFocus) 4.dp else 0.dp,
                                color = if (!isTvDevice && bannerHasFocus) Color.White else Color.Transparent,
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(if (isTvDevice) 16.dp else 12.dp)
                            )
                            .background(Color(0xFF101826))
                    ) {
                        if (playerKey == null) {
                            val backdrop = tmdbInfo?.backdropPath?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
                                ?.let { "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/${if (isTvDevice) "original" else "w1280"}$it" }
                                ?: selectedEpisode?.logo
                                ?: ""
                            if (backdrop.isNotEmpty()) {
                                val coverRequest = remember(backdrop, isTvDevice, seriesName) {
                                    ImageRequest.Builder(context)
                                        .data(backdrop)
                                        .size(if (isTvDevice) 1920 else 1280)
                                        .memoryCacheKey("${seriesName}_$backdrop")
                                        .crossfade(true)
                                        .build()
                                }
                                AsyncImage(
                                    model = coverRequest,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                    alignment = Alignment.TopCenter,
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                                )
                            } else {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxSize()
                                        .background(Color(0xFF101826)),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Text("SERIES", color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                                }
                            }
                            Box(
                                Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .background(
                                        if (isTvDevice) {
                                            Brush.verticalGradient(
                                                colorStops = arrayOf(
                                                    0f to Color.Transparent,
                                                    0.34f to Color.Transparent,
                                                    0.56f to Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                                                    0.78f to Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.62f),
                                                    1f to Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.96f)
                                                )
                                            )
                                        } else {
                                            Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(Color.Transparent, Color.Black.copy(0.85f)))
                                        }
                                    )
                            )
                            if (isTvDevice) {
                                Box(
                                    Modifier
                                        .fillMaxSize()
                                        .background(
                                            Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                                listOf(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f), Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.22f), Color.Transparent)
                                            )
                                        )
                                )
                                Column(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .align(Alignment.BottomStart)
                                        .padding(start = 32.dp, end = 32.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
                                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.Start
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = seriesName,
                                        color = Color.White,
                                        fontSize = 28.sp,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        maxLines = 2,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = "T${selectedEpisode?.seasonNum ?: 1} · E${selectedEpisode?.episodeNum ?: 1}  ${selectedEpisode?.title.orEmpty()}",
                                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.88f),
                                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    val episodeTags = listOfNotNull(
                                        selectedEpisode?.language?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() },
                                        selectedEpisode?.quality?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                                    )
                                    if (episodeTags.isNotEmpty()) {
                                        Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                            episodeTags.forEach { tag ->
                                                Text(
                                                    text = tag,
                                                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f),
                                                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                                    modifier = Modifier
                                                        .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                                        .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                    if (seriesSynopsis.isNotBlank()) {
                                        Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                        Text(
                                            text = seriesSynopsis,
                                            color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.94f),
                                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                                            lineHeight = 19.sp,
                                            maxLines = 2,
                                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(0.72f)
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                                    Row(
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Button(
                                            onClick = { if (!isExtractingPlay) openEpisodeFullscreen() },
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .focusProperties {
                                                    up = backFocusRequester
                                                    if (isTvDevice) down = serverSelectorFocusRequester
                                                }
                                                .focusRequester(playFocusRequester)
                                                .onFocusChanged { playButtonHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                                .graphicsLayer { scaleX = playButtonScale; scaleY = playButtonScale }
                                                .zIndex(if (playButtonHasFocus) 1f else 0f),
                                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                                containerColor = if (resumePositionMs > 0L) Color(0xFFE50914) else Color.White,
                                                contentColor = if (resumePositionMs > 0L) Color.White else Color.Black
                                            )
                                        ) {
                                            if (isExtractingPlay) {
                                                CircularProgressIndicator(
                                                    color = if (resumePositionMs > 0L) Color.White else Color.Black,
                                                    modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp),
                                                    strokeWidth = 2.dp
                                                )
                                            } else {
                                                Icon(Icons.Default.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                                            }
                                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                            Text(
                                                text = when {
                                                    isExtractingPlay -> "Conectando…"
                                                    resumePositionMs > 0L -> "Reanudar"
                                                    else -> "Reproducir episodio"
                                                },
                                                fontSize = 16.sp,
                                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                            )
                                        }
                                        Button(
                                            onClick = {
                                                isInMyList = M3uMyListStore.toggle(
                                                    context,
                                                    M3uMyListEntry(
                                                        mediaType = M3uMyListEntry.SERIES,
                                                        mediaId = seriesName,
                                                        title = seriesName,
                                                        logo = selectedEpisode?.logo.orEmpty(),
                                                        year = selectedEpisode?.year.orEmpty(),
                                                        language = selectedEpisode?.language.orEmpty(),
                                                        quality = selectedEpisode?.quality.orEmpty()
                                                    )
                                                )
                                                onMyListChanged()
                                            },
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .onFocusChanged { myListButtonHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                                .graphicsLayer { scaleX = myListButtonScale; scaleY = myListButtonScale }
                                                .zIndex(if (myListButtonHasFocus) 1f else 0f),
                                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = myListButtonColor, contentColor = Color.White)
                                        ) {
                                            Icon(if (isInMyList) Icons.Default.Check else Icons.Default.Add, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                                            Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                            Text(if (isInMyList) "En Mi lista" else "Mi lista", fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                        }
                                        if (seriesSynopsis.isNotBlank()) {
                                            TextButton(
                                                onClick = { showFullSynopsis = true },
                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                    .onFocusChanged { synopsisButtonHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                                    .graphicsLayer { scaleX = synopsisButtonScale; scaleY = synopsisButtonScale }
                                                    .zIndex(if (synopsisButtonHasFocus) 1f else 0f),
                                                colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = Color.White)
                                            ) {
                                                Text("Ver sinopsis completa", fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            } else {
                                Column(
                                    modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                                ) {
                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .size(72.dp)
                                            .animatedCircleGlow(isActive = bannerHasFocus && resumePositionMs <= 0L && !isTvDevice, color1 = Color(0xFFFF4500), color2 = Color(0xFFFFD700))
                                            .clip(CircleShape)
                                            .background(
                                                Brush.radialGradient(
                                                    colors = if (resumePositionMs > 0L) {
                                                        listOf(Color(0xFFE50914), Color(0xFFB20710))
                                                    } else {
                                                        listOf(Color(0xFFFFD700), Color(0xFFFF4500))
                                                    }
                                                )
                                            ),
                                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                    ) {
                                        if (isExtractingPlay) {
                                            CircularProgressIndicator(
                                                color = Color.White,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp),
                                                strokeWidth = 3.dp
                                            )
                                        } else {
                                            Icon(
                                                Icons.Default.PlayArrow,
                                                contentDescription = "Reproducir",
                                                tint = Color.White,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp)
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = if (resumePositionMs > 0L) "REANUDAR" else "VER EPISODIO",
                                        color = Color.White,
                                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        letterSpacing = 1.5.sp
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                    ) {
                        var serverSelectorHasFocus by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                        val serverSelectorScale by animateFloatAsState(
                            targetValue = if (isTvDevice && serverSelectorHasFocus) 1.03f else 1f,
                            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                            label = "series_server_selector_focus_scale"
                        )
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(52.dp)
                                .graphicsLayer { scaleX = serverSelectorScale; scaleY = serverSelectorScale }
                                .zIndex(if (serverSelectorHasFocus) 1f else 0f)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                .background(Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(Color(0xFF1E2A3A), Color(0xFF111827))))
                                .border(
                                    width = if (isTvDevice && serverSelectorHasFocus) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                                    color = if (isTvDevice && serverSelectorHasFocus) Color.White else Color(0xFF3B82F6).copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                                )
                                .focusRequester(serverSelectorFocusRequester)
                                .focusProperties {
                                    if (isTvDevice) {
                                        up = playFocusRequester
                                        down = if (seasonNames.isNotEmpty()) seasonSelectorFocusRequester else firstEpisodeFocusRequester
                                    }
                                }
                                .onKeyEvent { event ->
                                    if (isTvDevice && event.type == KeyEventType.KeyDown && event.key == Key.DirectionDown) {
                                        if (seasonNames.isNotEmpty()) {
                                            runCatching { seasonSelectorFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
                                        } else {
                                            focusEpisodeAt(0)
                                        }
                                        true
                                    } else {
                                        false
                                    }
                                }
                                .onFocusChanged { serverSelectorHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                .clickable(
                                    interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                                    indication = null,
                                    enabled = servers.isNotEmpty(),
                                    onClick = { expandedServers = true }
                                )
                                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    Icons.Rounded.Dns,
                                    null,
                                    tint = Color(0xFF3B82F6),
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                )
                                Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))

                                val currentServer = selectedServer
                                val lang = if (!currentServer?.language.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                    " (${currentServer?.language})"
                                } else ""

                                Text(
                                    text = if (currentServer != null) {
                                        "Servidor ${servers.indexOf(currentServer) + 1}: ${currentServer.name}$lang"
                                    } else {
                                        "Sin servidores"
                                    },
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    fontSize = 15.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                )
                                Text("▼", color = Color.Gray, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                        }

                        DropdownMenu(
                            expanded = expandedServers,
                            onDismissRequest = { expandedServers = false },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .background(Color(0xFF1E2A3A))
                                .fillMaxWidth(0.9f)
                        ) {
                            servers.forEachIndexed { index, srv ->
                                val lang = if (!srv.language.isNullOrBlank()) " (${srv.language})" else ""
                                DropdownMenuItem(
                                    text = {
                                        Text(
                                            "Servidor ${index + 1}: ${srv.name}$lang",
                                            color = if (srv == selectedServer) Color(0xFF3B82F6) else Color.White
                                        )
                                    },
                                    onClick = {
                                        if (srv != selectedServer) {
                                            selectedServer = srv
                                            expandedServers = false
                                            retryCount = 0

                                            if (playerKey != null && selectedEpisode != null) {
                                                currentStreamUrl = null
                                                playerKey = null
                                                isSilentMode = false
                                                VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
                                                VodPlayerHolder.player = null

                                                CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                                                    delay(150)
                                                    openEpisodeServer(
                                                        episode = selectedEpisode!!,
                                                        server = srv,
                                                        saveRecent = false
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            } else {
                                                expandedServers = false
                                            }
                                        } else {
                                            expandedServers = false
                                        }
                                    }
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    if (!isTvDevice) {
                        Text(
                            text = seriesName,
                            color = Color.White,
                            fontSize = 24.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                        )

                        Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))

                        Text(
                            text = "Estás viendo: ${selectedEpisode?.title ?: ""}",
                            color = Color(0xFFCFD8DC),
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                        )

                        Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    }

                    if (!isTvDevice) {
                        val mobileMyListColor by animateColorAsState(
                            targetValue = if (isInMyList) Color(0xFF2E6B4F) else Color(0xFF414A56),
                            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220),
                            label = "series_mobile_my_list_color"
                        )
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                selectedEpisode?.language?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let {
                                    AssistChip(onClick = {}, label = { Text(it, fontSize = 10.sp) })
                                }
                                selectedEpisode?.quality?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let {
                                    AssistChip(onClick = {}, label = { Text(it, fontSize = 10.sp) })
                                }
                            }
                            Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                            TextButton(
                                onClick = {
                                    isInMyList = M3uMyListStore.toggle(
                                        context,
                                        M3uMyListEntry(
                                            mediaType = M3uMyListEntry.SERIES,
                                            mediaId = seriesName,
                                            title = seriesName,
                                            logo = selectedEpisode?.logo.orEmpty(),
                                            year = selectedEpisode?.year.orEmpty(),
                                            language = selectedEpisode?.language.orEmpty(),
                                            quality = selectedEpisode?.quality.orEmpty()
                                        )
                                    )
                                    onMyListChanged()
                                },
                                colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = mobileMyListColor),
                                contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                            ) {
                                Icon(if (isInMyList) Icons.Default.Check else Icons.Default.Add, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                                Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text(if (isInMyList) "En Mi lista" else "Mi lista", fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                            }
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            val isWifi = isWifiConnected(context)
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = {
                                    if (currentStreamUrl != null && playerKey != null) {
                                        castStreamUrl = currentStreamUrl
                                        castReferer = currentReferer
                                        castCookies = preExtractedCookies
                                        showCastDialog = true
                                    } else {
                                        if (selectedServer != null && selectedEpisode != null) {
                                            openEpisodeServer(
                                                episode = selectedEpisode!!,
                                                server = selectedServer!!,
                                                saveRecent = false,
                                                isForCast = true
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                },
                                enabled = !isExtractingCast && isWifi,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(40.dp)
                                    .background(Color(0xFF1E2A3A), CircleShape)
                            ) {
                                if (isExtractingCast) {
                                    CircularProgressIndicator(color = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                                } else {
                                    Icon(Icons.Default.CastConnected, contentDescription = "Transmitir a TV", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    val sinopsis = seriesSynopsis
                    if (!isTvDevice && (sinopsis.isNotBlank() || !selectedEpisode?.logo.isNullOrBlank())) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
                        ) {
                            val posterUrl = selectedEpisode?.logo ?: tmdbInfo?.posterPath?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w342$it" } ?: ""
                            if (posterUrl.isNotEmpty()) {
                                val posterRequest = remember(posterUrl) {
                                    ImageRequest.Builder(context)
                                        .data(posterUrl)
                                        .size(150)
                                        .memoryCacheKey(posterUrl)
                                        .crossfade(false)
                                        .build()
                                }
                                AsyncImage(
                                    model = posterRequest,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(90.dp, 130.dp)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                )
                                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                            }
                            if (sinopsis.isNotBlank()) {
                                var synopsisTruncated by remember(seriesName, sinopsis) { mutableStateOf(false) }
                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = sinopsis,
                                        color = Color(0xFFB0BEC5),
                                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                                        lineHeight = 22.sp,
                                        maxLines = 4,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                        onTextLayout = { synopsisTruncated = it.hasVisualOverflow }
                                    )
                                    if (synopsisTruncated) {
                                        TextButton(
                                            onClick = { showFullSynopsis = true },
                                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 0.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                        ) {
                                            Text("Ver completo", color = Color(0xFF60A5FA), fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    }

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))

                    if (seasonNames.isNotEmpty()) {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Temporadas",
                                color = Color.White,
                                fontSize = 16.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 8.dp)
                            )

                            var menuExpanded by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                            val selectedText = remember(selectedSeasonName, episodesCountBySeason) {
                                val count = episodesCountBySeason[selectedSeasonName] ?: 0
                                "$selectedSeasonName - $count cap"
                            }

                            var seasonSelectorHasFocus by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                            val seasonSelectorScale by animateFloatAsState(
                                targetValue = if (isTvDevice && seasonSelectorHasFocus) 1.03f else 1f,
                                animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                                label = "series_season_selector_focus_scale"
                            )
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .height(52.dp)
                                    .graphicsLayer { scaleX = seasonSelectorScale; scaleY = seasonSelectorScale }
                                    .zIndex(if (seasonSelectorHasFocus) 1f else 0f)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(Color(0xFF1E2A3A))
                                    .border(
                                        width = if (isTvDevice && seasonSelectorHasFocus) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                                        color = if (isTvDevice && seasonSelectorHasFocus) Color.White else Color(0xFF3B82F6).copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                                    )
                                    .focusRequester(seasonSelectorFocusRequester)
                                    .focusProperties {
                                        if (isTvDevice) {
                                            up = serverSelectorFocusRequester
                                            down = firstEpisodeFocusRequester
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .onKeyEvent { event ->
                                        if (isTvDevice && event.type == KeyEventType.KeyDown &&
                                            event.key == Key.DirectionDown &&
                                            groupedBySeason[selectedSeasonName].orEmpty().isNotEmpty()
                                        ) {
                                            focusEpisodeAt(0)
                                            true
                                        } else {
                                            false
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .onFocusChanged { seasonSelectorHasFocus = it.isFocused }
                                    .clickable(
                                        interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                                        indication = null,
                                        onClick = { menuExpanded = true }
                                    )
                                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = selectedText,
                                        color = Color.White,
                                        fontSize = 15.sp,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                    )
                                    Icon(
                                        Icons.Default.KeyboardArrowDown,
                                        contentDescription = "Abrir temporadas",
                                        tint = Color(0xFF3B82F6),
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }

                            DropdownMenu(
                                expanded = menuExpanded,
                                onDismissRequest = { menuExpanded = false },
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .background(Color(0xFF1E2A3A))
                                    .fillMaxWidth(0.9f)
                            ) {
                                seasonNames.forEach { season ->
                                    val count = episodesCountBySeason[season] ?: 0
                                    val display = "$season - $count cap"
                                    DropdownMenuItem(
                                        text = {
                                            Text(
                                                display,
                                                color = if (season == selectedSeasonName) Color(0xFF3B82F6) else Color.White,
                                                fontWeight = if (season == selectedSeasonName) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                                            )
                                        },
                                        onClick = {
                                            selectedSeasonName = season
                                            menuExpanded = false
                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    Text(
                        text = "Episodios",
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontSize = 18.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                    )

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                }

                val episodesOfSelectedSeason = groupedBySeason[selectedSeasonName] ?: emptyList()

                items(episodesOfSelectedSeason, key = { episode -> "${episode.url}_${episode.seasonNum}_${episode.episodeNum}" }, contentType = { "episode_item" }) { episode ->
                    val episodeIndex = episodesOfSelectedSeason.indexOfFirst {
                        episodeFocusKey(it) == episodeFocusKey(episode)
                    }
                    val episodeRequester = if (episodeIndex == 0) {
                        firstEpisodeFocusRequester
                    } else {
                        episodeFocusRequesters.getOrPut(episodeFocusKey(episode)) { FocusRequester() }
                    }
                    EpisodeItem(
                        episode = episode,
                        isSelected = selectedEpisode?.url == episode.url,
                        listId = listId,
                        focusRequester = episodeRequester,
                        onMoveUp = {
                            if (episodeIndex > 0) {
                                focusEpisodeAt(episodeIndex - 1)
                            } else if (seasonNames.isNotEmpty()) {
                                runCatching { seasonSelectorFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
                            } else {
                                runCatching { serverSelectorFocusRequester.requestFocus() }
                            }
                        },
                        onMoveDown = {
                            if (episodeIndex >= 0 && episodeIndex < episodesOfSelectedSeason.lastIndex) {
                                focusEpisodeAt(episodeIndex + 1)
                            }
                        },
                        onClick = {
                            selectedEpisode = episode
                            selectedServer = episode.servers.ifEmpty {
                                listOf(M3uServer("Servidor Único", episode.url, episode.isEmbed, episode.language))
                            }.firstOrNull()

                            if (playerKey != null) {
                                val srv = selectedServer ?: M3uServer(
                                    "Servidor",
                                    episode.url,
                                    episode.isEmbed,
                                    episode.language
                                )

                                currentEpTitle = episode.title
                                currentStreamUrl = null
                                playerKey = null
                                VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
                                VodPlayerHolder.player = null

                                scope.launch {
                                    delay(100)
                                    openEpisodeServer(
                                        episode = episode,
                                        server = srv,
                                        saveRecent = true
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    )
                }

                item {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(32.dp))
                }
            }
        }

        if (showFullSynopsis && seriesSynopsis.isNotBlank()) {
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { showFullSynopsis = false },
                title = { Text("Sinopsis completa", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
                text = {
                    Text(
                        text = seriesSynopsis,
                        color = Color(0xFFCFD8DC),
                        modifier = Modifier.heightIn(max = 360.dp).verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                    )
                },
                confirmButton = { TextButton(onClick = { showFullSynopsis = false }) { Text("Cerrar") } },
                containerColor = Color(0xFF1E2A3A),
                titleContentColor = Color.White,
                textContentColor = Color(0xFFCFD8DC)
            )
        }

        if (showCastDialog && castStreamUrl != null) {
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { showCastDialog = false },
                title = { Text("Transmisión Lista", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
                text = { Text("El video ha sido extraído correctamente. ¿Deseas abrir Web Video Caster para enviarlo a tu TV?") },
                confirmButton = {
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            showCastDialog = false
                            ExternalCaster.castViaWebcaster(
                                context = context,
                                videoUrl = castStreamUrl!!,
                                title = currentEpTitle.ifEmpty { selectedEpisode?.title ?: "" },
                                referer = castReferer,
                                cookies = castCookies
                            )
                        },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFF3B82F6))
                    ) { Text("Aceptar", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }
                },
                dismissButton = {
                    TextButton(onClick = { showCastDialog = false }) { Text("Cancelar", color = Color.LightGray) }
                },
                containerColor = Color(0xFF1E2A3A),
                titleContentColor = Color.White,
                textContentColor = Color(0xFFCFD8DC)
            )
        }

        if (playerKey != null && currentStreamUrl != null && selectedEpisode != null) {
            val playerModifier = if (isFullscreen) {
                Modifier.fillMaxSize().zIndex(100f)
            } else {
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth().aspectRatio(16f / 9f).align(Alignment.TopCenter).zIndex(100f)
            }

            key(playerKey) {
                Box(modifier = playerModifier) {
                    when (activePlayerType) {
                        1 -> {
                            ExoVideoPlayer(
                                url = currentStreamUrl!!,
                                referer = currentReferer,
                                channelName = currentEpTitle,
                                resumePositionMs = resumePositionMs,
                                preExtractedCookies = preExtractedCookies,
                                isSeries = true,
                                episodesList = groupedBySeason[selectedSeasonName] ?: emptyList(),
                                currentEpisodeUrl = selectedEpisode?.url ?: "",
                                onEpisodeSelected = { episodeUrl ->
                                    val selectedEp = sortedEpisodes.firstOrNull { it.url == episodeUrl }
                                    if (selectedEp != null && selectedEp.url != selectedEpisode?.url) {
                                        selectedEpisode = selectedEp
                                        selectedServer = selectedEp.servers.ifEmpty {
                                            listOf(M3uServer("Servidor Único", selectedEp.url, selectedEp.isEmbed, selectedEp.language))
                                        }.firstOrNull()

                                        if (selectedServer != null) {
                                            currentEpTitle = selectedEp.title
                                            currentStreamUrl = null
                                            playerKey = null
                                            isSilentMode = false
                                            VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
                                            VodPlayerHolder.player = null

                                            scope.launch {
                                                delay(150)
                                                openEpisodeServer(
                                                    episode = selectedEp,
                                                    server = selectedServer!!,
                                                    saveRecent = true
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                },
                                originalEmbedUrl = selectedServer?.url ?: "",
                                seriesName = seriesName,
                                seasonNumber = selectedEpisode?.seasonNum,
                                episodeNumber = selectedEpisode?.episodeNum,
                                serverName = selectedServer?.name ?: "",
                                sharedViewModel = sharedCatalogViewModel,
                                refererFallback = currentRefererFallback ?: "",
                                onHardHttpError = { code ->
                                    // ✅ REPORTAR ERROR HTTP DURO EN EXOPLAYER
                                    val epUrl = selectedEpisode?.url ?: ""
                                    val srvUrl = selectedServer?.url ?: ""
                                    if (epUrl.isNotBlank() && srvUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                                        Log.w("SERIES_EMBED", "⚠️ Error HTTP duro $code en ExoPlayer. Reportando...")
                                        SupabaseFailReporter.reportCanalFail(context, epUrl, srvUrl)
                                    }
                                },
                                onProgressUpdate = { positionMs, durationMs ->
                                    val ep = selectedEpisode ?: return@ExoVideoPlayer
                                    M3uWatchHistoryStore.updatePosition(
                                        contentUrl = ep.url,
                                        watchType = M3uWatchType.SERIES,
                                        positionMs = positionMs,
                                        durationMs = durationMs
                                    )
                                },
                                onBackClick = {
                                    if (isFullscreen && !isTvDevice) {
                                        leaveFullscreenAndKeepPlayback()
                                    } else {
                                        stopPlaybackAndReturnToEpisodes()
                                    }
                                },
                                onNextEpisode = {
                                    val currentEp = selectedEpisode ?: return@ExoVideoPlayer
                                    val currentSeason = groupedBySeason[selectedSeasonName] ?: emptyList()
                                    val currentIndex = currentSeason.indexOfFirst { it.url == currentEp.url }

                                    if (currentIndex >= 0 && currentIndex < currentSeason.size - 1) {
                                        val nextEp = currentSeason[currentIndex + 1]
                                        val nextServer = nextEp.servers.ifEmpty {
                                            listOf(M3uServer("Servidor Único", nextEp.url, nextEp.isEmbed, nextEp.language))
                                        }.firstOrNull()

                                        if (nextServer != null) {
                                            selectedEpisode = nextEp
                                            selectedServer = nextServer
                                            currentEpTitle = nextEp.title
                                            currentStreamUrl = null
                                            playerKey = null
                                            isSilentMode = false
                                            VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
                                            VodPlayerHolder.player = null

                                            scope.launch {
                                                delay(150)
                                                openEpisodeServer(
                                                    episode = nextEp,
                                                    server = nextServer,
                                                    saveRecent = true
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    } else {
                                        val currentSeasonIndex = seasonNames.indexOfFirst { it == selectedSeasonName }
                                        if (currentSeasonIndex >= 0 && currentSeasonIndex < seasonNames.size - 1) {
                                            val nextSeasonName = seasonNames[currentSeasonIndex + 1]
                                            val nextSeasonEpisodes = groupedBySeason[nextSeasonName] ?: emptyList()
                                            val nextEp = nextSeasonEpisodes.firstOrNull()
                                            val nextServer = nextEp?.servers?.firstOrNull()

                                            if (nextEp != null && nextServer != null) {
                                                selectedSeasonName = nextSeasonName
                                                selectedEpisode = nextEp
                                                selectedServer = nextServer
                                                currentEpTitle = nextEp.title
                                                currentStreamUrl = null
                                                playerKey = null
                                                isSilentMode = false
                                                VodPlayerHolder.player?.release()
                                                VodPlayerHolder.player = null

                                                scope.launch {
                                                    delay(150)
                                                    openEpisodeServer(
                                                        episode = nextEp,
                                                        server = nextServer,
                                                        saveRecent = true
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            }
                                        } else {
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "Has llegado al último capítulo", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            )
                        }
                        2 -> {
                            AppVideoPlayer(
                                streamUrl = currentStreamUrl,
                                isLive = false,
                                channelName = currentEpTitle,
                                resumePositionMs = resumePositionMs,
                                trackState = trackState,
                                referer = currentReferer,
                                onError = { triggerServerFallback() },
                                onProgressUpdate = { positionMs, durationMs ->
                                    val ep = selectedEpisode ?: return@AppVideoPlayer
                                    M3uWatchHistoryStore.updatePosition(
                                        contentUrl = ep.url,
                                        watchType = M3uWatchType.SERIES,
                                        positionMs = positionMs,
                                        durationMs = durationMs
                                    )
                                },
                                onBackClick = {
                                    if (isFullscreen && !isTvDevice) {
                                        leaveFullscreenAndKeepPlayback()
                                    } else {
                                        stopPlaybackAndReturnToEpisodes()
                                    }
                                }
                            )
                        }
                        3 -> {
                            EmbedVideoPlayer(
                                url = currentStreamUrl!!,
                                canalUrl = selectedEpisode?.url ?: "",
                                isSilentMode = isSilentMode,
                                fanartUrl = "",
                                refererOverride = currentReferer,     // ═══ NUEVO: pasar Referer al iframe ═══
                                onTimeoutError = {
                                    // ✅ MOSTRAR OVERLAY Y FORZAR CAMBIO DE SERVIDOR
                                    val srvName = selectedServer?.name ?: "desconocido"
                                    Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Timeout o Video no encontrado en: $srvName")
                                    serverDeadMessageText = "El servidor \"$srvName\" no está disponible.\nCambiando de fuente…"
                                    showServerDeadOverlay = true
                                    CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                                        delay(1500)
                                        showServerDeadOverlay = false
                                        forceNextServer()
                                    }
                                },
                                onEmbedUnavailable = {
                                    Log.w("VOD_EXTRACT", "⚠️ Servidor caído detectado: ${selectedServer?.name ?: "desconocido"}")

                                    val epUrl = selectedEpisode?.url ?: ""
                                    val srvUrl = selectedServer?.url ?: ""
                                    if (epUrl.isNotBlank() && srvUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                                        SupabaseFailReporter.reportCanalFail(context, epUrl, srvUrl)
                                    }

                                    serverDeadMessageText = "El servidor \"${selectedServer?.name ?: "desconocido"}\" no está disponible.\nCambiando de fuente…"
                                    showServerDeadOverlay = true
                                    CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                                        delay(1500)
                                        showServerDeadOverlay = false
                                        forceNextServer()
                                    }
                                },
                                onM3u8Intercepted = { interceptedUrl, cookies ->
                                    Log.d("SERIES_EMBED", "🔍 Validando stream: $interceptedUrl")

                                    val isDoodstreamStream =
                                        interceptedUrl.contains("token=", ignoreCase = true) && (
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("cloudatacdn.com", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("dood.video", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("doodcdn.io", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                        interceptedUrl.contains("ds2play.com", ignoreCase = true)
                                                )

                                    val isIbraStream =
                                        interceptedUrl.contains("ibra.lat", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                                interceptedUrl.contains("cloudwindow-route.com", ignoreCase = true)

                                    val isIbraMaster = isIbraStream && interceptedUrl.contains("?s=", ignoreCase = true)
                                    val isIbraVariantM3u8 = isIbraStream && interceptedUrl.substringBefore("?").endsWith(".m3u8", ignoreCase = true)

                                    val isValidStream = isIbraStream || isDoodstreamStream ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("master.m3u8", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("master.txt", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains(".urlset/", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                            interceptedUrl.contains("hls2-c", ignoreCase = true) ||
